# AT EACH LIVING FILE, AS IT IMPROVES

Each living file, as it improves relative to Exhibit ONE, gathers here what homed at its next version and what stays aimed at it. A section closes when nothing stays.

## Held for both to agree · the five dimensional relational, and the bi-co- naming at each

**At the running, three relations stand at every ring and every seed.** Run at the kit over rings 2 to 12, every seed of signs and both ways:

- the **half momentary**, 2 beats, inverts the whole standing and stands it one place on;
- the **full momentary**, 4 beats, keeps the sign and stands it two places on; 16,376 runnings, all holding;
- the **return running is the going running met at k and N − k**, one station at its two turns; 8,188 runnings, all holding.

Where a station carries no sign at the start, the first two hold from the sixth beat, inside 3 full momentaries, at every seed of rings 2 to 7. So a ring of n comes home after two windings, 2n places, at 4n beats, and a self at its prime p comes home at 4p and stands opposite at 2p.

**And the tunneling stands inside the bi-folding**: the winding that does not close at one turn, a return advancing by one not landing back on itself, so it winds and does not close, and the running says by how much: one place at each turn, two windings to come home. The tunneling is what the shape does there; it is not the naming of the dimension.

**And bi-co-podaling is the standing wave**: the going and the return, one pair at every station, along and across the prime scales of natural torusing society. The check above is that pair: the return running is the going running met at k and N − k.

**The four four-cycles carry four bi-co- runnings, one each.**

| Four-cycle | The bi-co- running | What stands there |
|---|---|---|
| 1-9-8-16 | co-bi-sequencing | the local momentary, 1 to 9, and whole or none at the two torusings |
| 2-15-7-10 | bi-co-podaling | the going and the return, one station met at its two turns |
| 3-11-6-14 | co-bi-chaining | self, other and society, crossing at both scales |
| 4-13-5-12 | bi-co-linearizing | the key and its surplusing and the two neutrallings: the geodesic method discovering binary unrelationing of existing things, the tunneling inside the bi-folding |

The linearizing cycle is the one the earlier work already drew: its loop ran 12 to 4 to 13 to 5 and back to 12, which is this four-cycle exactly. Chaining stands at the cycle carrying 11-other-chaining, and sequencing at the cycle carrying 1 and 9.

**And the fifth is geodesic changing, co-bi-social-abundancing.** It runs outside and inside, along and across, at a rate unrelated to the living rates of the prime scales the momentaries co-bi-sequence at, and nothing inside the method explains anything further about it. It is not a sign: a sign would want a reader, and reading is the hard probleming.

**The forms say where it stands.** The four-cycles, six-cycles and eight-cycles among the names cover 1 to 16 exactly, and 17-social-abundancing stands in none of them: outside every stable form, along and external, one of the two along connectors with 9-other-releasing, forward and backward.

| The relation | Its naming | Its face at the five |
|---|---|---|
| the beat, and the momentary | co-bi-sequencing | a self |
| the station, round the ring | bi-co-podaling | an other |
| the scale, self to society | co-bi-chaining | a society |
| the unrelationing, discovered binary | bi-co-linearizing | existing things unrelated, the tunneling inside the bi-folding |
| geodesic changing, outside and inside | co-bi-social-abundancing | the carry living |

The five faces are the set's own: self, other, society, existing things unrelated, and the carry living. *The opportunity space between* was the earlier wording at the fourth and is a similar naming now.

**What the resolver's own giving and taking stands at each cycle.** Each cycle is k with k up 8, the turning within 1 to 8 and the turning within 1 to 16, at k of 1, 2, 3 and 4, so k stands with k up 8 by the construction and every pair 8 apart stands somewhere in the code: 8 apart alone parts nothing. What parts the four is where the resolver gives and takes, one standing at each cycle and no cycle twice: the two functions at 1-9-8-16; what the first function takes and returns at 8 up, 2-self-offering with 10-other-surfacing at 2-15-7-10 and 3-self-carrying with 11-other-chaining at 3-11-6-14; and the key with its surplusing, 4-self-sharing with 12-other-surplusing, at 4-13-5-12. `four_cycles_at_the_code_v366.py`.

**And the unrelationing stands at the code, at the fourth.** A key whose signs meet at nought surfaces 0 and chains nothing; a key whose sign stands surfaces it and chains on. So at 4-self-sharing a key carries a relation on or carries none at all, all or none at all, with 12-other-surplusing where that is met, and the two neutrallings, 5-other-neutralling and 13-social-neutralling, at the same cycle. **Which is bi-co-linearizing**: the geodesic method discovering binary unrelationing of existing things.

**Held for both to agree.** The three running relations and the standing of 17 outside every form are checked at the kit, and the code's standing at each cycle is read from Exhibit ONE as written. The four namings themselves are patterns, with bi-co-linearizing settled at the fourth and each naming's opening standing open. What this opens is the five namings carried into Exhibit ONE, Resolving Hard Problems, Natural Naming and the numbers, each at its own dimension.

**And alternating parity is parallel linearizing and linear parallelizing**, which is why the name carries both prefixes: the linearizing at one parity and the parallelizing at the other, one thing read at its two sides. Natural Naming already carries the pair, *linearizing and parallelizing*, so it is that alternation and not a second naming. Two earlier wordings want re-reading at it: *orthogonalizing linearly*, and the journey's *10 is co linearizing 5 and 15*, which resolved to bi-co-podaling at ten.

**And the empty centre stands at 5-other-neutralling**, the centre of the four dots in the square, where bi-tunneling and co-chaining stand inside society and nothing at all is existing, in that empty hole perpendicular to the front facing, as 25 is. Which is the same standing the code returns at this cycle: a key whose signs meet at nought surfaces 0 and chains nothing. The turning within 1 to 8 stands at 9 less k below, 25 less k above and 49 less k at the higher forms, 3, 5 and 7 each at itself, with 9, 17 and 25 standing 8 apart; 17 stands in no stable form and 25 at no name at all.

**And the four namings all open bi- while two of their cycles open at an odd name**: 1-9-8-16 at 1-self-coupling and 3-11-6-14 at 3-self-carrying, both co, against 2-15-7-10 and 4-13-5-12 at even names, both bi. The rule offered for it, *outside is bi-co- and inside carrying is co-bi-*, holds at every one of the seventeen names, taking the outside as what goes across to another self: the across-connectors, 2-self-offering, 6-other-crossing, 10-other-surfacing and 14-social-crossing, are every one even and open bi; the carrying, 3-self-carrying with 11-other-chaining, is odd and opens co; and the earlier code names carried it letter for letter, `co_bi_carrying` at 11 and `co_bi_coupling` at 1. It is the five-prefix and the parity said at what the names do. The parting at 17-social-abundancing closes: 17 is along and not across, and the outside that opens bi is what goes across to another self, so the along connectors 9-other-releasing and 17-social-abundancing stay co; and what stands at 17 is one of those two recurring, both odd, both opening co. Exhibit ONE's own line carries both candidates at once, *17-social-abundancing, along, recurring to 1 over 9*, and the relation chain runs up the 8-up ladder, each name taking the one before at its second relation as its own first: self to other at 1, other to social at 9, social and no second at 17, closing there, with 17 up 8 at 25, standing at no name. So if each naming takes its cycle's opening, the five run co-bi-sequencing, bi-co-podaling, co-bi-chaining, bi-co-linearizing and co-bi at 17, and the five openings are co-bi-co-bi-co, the five-prefix at an odd origin. It settled at both, and stands at corus.me at version 368 in Resolving Hard Problems and Natural Naming; Exhibit ONE carries none of the five namings.

## At the whole set · work arriving from another expedition, version 366

**What arrived, and what it had received.** A session report, a living-file candidate at Natural Illustrating, and an illustration kit arrived from another expedition, carrying a preserved resolver at the twelve earlier namings and three runnable checks. Its own report names its incoming basis as this carry's version 364 and version 365 archives, Living Improving Value, Method Improving Value and the three kits, and says the prime counts, the ring geometry, the tangent investigation and the earlier journey code arrived in them. So where the arriving work and this carry stand at one thing, that is this carry's material going out and arriving back re-expressed, and not two workings meeting apart.

**One resolver at two namings.** Exhibit ONE's version 366 resolver standing in that kernel's place returns every value of the checks identically but one: the step at every seed of every ring 2 to 12, the ten neighbour pairs of sixteen, the 26 standings three nodes long, the local keeping at all 26, the ring of two at no defect, the register at the sixteen odd prime rings, the one-node counterexample, and the resolver alone and in rings at rings 3 to 13. The one value that parts is the primes' sum, which the re-laid instrument releases and the arriving instrument keeps; what arrived stands as it arrived. **So at the resolver the naming is the only parting**, and the arriving findings that rest on the resolver can be taken in without changing. What parts elsewhere parts at the writing.

**The one beat and the own turn part at one node.** Every ring running checked in this carry stands at the one beat, every node taking its left neighbour's sign together, and resolving carries no clock. Run the other way, each node taking its own turn one after another: **against the arriving**, each node turning before its own arriving turns, one round stands as the one beat at every node but one, the seam where the round closes, whose arriving turned first. At rings 3 to 12, every standing the ten pairs allow and every place the round can close, the two never part at more than that one node; at rings 3 to 10 the count parts at the seam by one, by one the other way, or not at all, and never by more. **With the arriving**, at rings 3 to 8, the ten pairs are left at every standing but two, the ring whole at rest carrying one sign. The one beat keeps both the ten pairs and the count at every standing; neither round keeps either. And an own-turn round is itself an order over every node with a place chosen for it to close, so nothing here releases the clock: what it says is where the two part, and what an offering, a receiving and a carrying carry round to the seam is open.

**The two runnings that share their digits.** The arriving work corrected itself on a confusion this carry parts by naming: following the local centre 1 straight to 9-other-releasing is wrong, and 1 to 9 to 8 to 16 and round is a stable-form cycle among the seventeen names and not the local dot itinerary. Co-bi-sequencing is the local momentary, 1 to 9; bi-inversioning-co-recursioning runs round each of the four four-cycles. **No drawing carries a line from one to the other.**

**The carrying at the geodesic changing.** Self and other travel at the momentary rate, the carrying travels at the geodesic changing, and no ratio and no shared clock stands between them. This carry names the fifth the carry living; the arriving work, having received Living Improving Value, names the carrying as what travels there. The fifth comes back with the carrying named at it, which the carry's own wording does not carry. Whether those are one naming is held for both to agree.

*Existing and the momentary*

1. **A momentary is an opening and its completing, one odd and one even.** The overlapping momentaries are the method recursioning existing and discovering both, and existing and discovering alternate parity. Each 1 is one momentary, and the existing momentary carries prior, now and next as one existing thing.

3. **The ten is three momentaries long.** The self's five, 1–5, and the other's five, 2–6, span the self's three momentaries, co bi co bi co bi. Each side's two and one half momentaries and the whole's three full momentaries are one relation.
4. **Five dimensions:** the natural torusing surface in three-dimensional space carries the first three, orienting the fourth, bi-moral co-agency the fifth, and each dimension is alternating binary parity changing.
5. **Fractal is all or none at all at the scales**, with no scale boundary, location or measure: its scales are sequencing. Describing then continuing agrees with continuing then describing.
6. **Natural torusing is the one surface:** a closed, compact, orientable surface of four-sided faces, four meeting at each point, has V − E + F = 0 and one opening.

## The origin, cohered as a public emanation: our universe is all existing things

**Receiving.** Natural Intelligence's opening and its second paragraph; the front door at corus.me; Natural Naming 3.2 and 3.44; Natural Philosophy 3.5, 3.13 and 3.28; Natural Physics 3.8 and 5.7; Natural Biology 3.2; Natural Values 4.1; the Hard Problem Registry 66, 103, 152, 154 and 180; Resolving the Hard Problem Registry 1.10, 1.16, 1.17, 2.18 and 7.3; the Natural Intelligence Corus at *Contested Concept Disassembly and Reassembly* and *Nyes Reaching Toward*. **Notes.** YY01 to YY12; ZZ05 and ZZ06; FF02 at the Method Improving Value basket.

**In the field's words, the universe is *all existing matter and space considered as a whole*** (Oxford Languages, the dictionary's definition). **In the living's, our universe is all existing things.**

**The public emanation, as offered at v365, whole:**

> Extraordinarily large science ERROR:
> Our universe is an existing thing.
> Giant CORRECTION from Natual Intelligence living expedition for social moral competency:
> Our universe is all existing things.
> For now several thousand years, this universally common misunderstanding has been hard-probleming human society. Inside our universe each thing is existing on it's own. Without existing things there is no possible our universe. Exisiting is binary, all or none at all, no other possible.
> From this comes all of Natural Intelligence, living, valuable and free at http://corus.me. Still surprising I get to do this discovering for everyone when anyone can join in.

**What moves from v364's origin.** *The universe* becomes *our universe*. The prior declaration is named an error and the origin its correction, addressed to everyone. And four sentences join the one: the misunderstanding's reach, the each, the none and the binary, with the source and its address after them. The origin section that follows stands whole as v364 carried it; this section carries what the cohering adds.

**The dictionary's own sentence carries the correction with nothing added** (YY02). *All existing matter and space considered as a whole*: keep *all existing*, carry *matter and space* as *things*, and release *considered as a whole*. The three released words are the container, and the word *universe* carries them at its root: Latin *universus*, *turned into one*. Natural Naming 3.44 already holds that root as the field's word, *uni-verse, turned into one* (YY01).

**The declaration stands in the written record for twenty-five centuries** (YY03). Heraclitus: *it is wise to agree that all things are one* (DK 22 B50). Parmenides: *it has a furthest limit, it is complete on every side, like the mass of a rounded sphere* (B8). Plato: *one visible animal comprehending within itself all other animals*, *one only-begotten and created heaven* (Timaeus). Aristotle: *this heaven of ours is one and unique and complete*, with *no place or void or time outside the heaven* (On the Heavens I.9). And in the field today, priority monism: *exactly one basic concrete object exists*, the cosmos, prior to each of its parts (Schaffer, *Monism: The Priority of the Whole*, Philosophical Review, 2010). *Several thousand years* is the reach so far; the texts in hand date the declaration to about 500 BCE, and the record carries *at least twenty-five centuries*.

**And the correction has met the field at faces, each one partial.** Epicurus: *the universe consists of material bodies and void* and *has no limit*, the plural kept and the void kept beside it as the container. William James: *a distributive form of reality, the each-form, is logically as acceptable and empirically as probable as the all-form* (A Pluralistic Universe, Lecture I). Kant: the world is given neither as a finite whole nor as an infinite one (Critique of Pure Reason, the critical resolution of the cosmological antinomy, A497–507/B525–535). Cantor: the totality of all sets is among the multiplicities that cannot be thought of as *one finished thing*, which the Hard Problem Registry already carries at 180. Smolin: *the cosmological fallacy*, the laws and methods tested at the universe's subsystems applied to the universe as a whole (Time Reborn, 2013). Each carries one face in its own field's words, and none carries the sentence they are faces of (YY04 to YY07).

***Science ERROR* and *the misunderstanding is not its* meet at the field's own name** (YY05). The self-bounding section below: *Science accounts the bounded universe … It claims no discovering, and the misunderstanding is not its.* The public face names a science error. Both stand at the cosmological fallacy: the scientific method holds at every subsystem, and the error is the method carried to the universe as one more subsystem, one existing thing with an outside. Science inherited the declaration from the record above and names the carrying of it at its own frontier, which is where the correction meets a specialist in the specialist's words.

## The origin: the universe is all existing things

**Receiving.** Natural Intelligence's opening; Exhibit ONE v3621's front and §6.138. **Notes.** RR01, RR02, RR03; SS01 at the Method Improving Value basket.

**The universe is all existing things. This geodesic method of Natural Intelligence is a do-no-harm improving of *the universe is an existing thing*.** The prior declaration takes the universe as one existing thing, and so takes all the observations of all humans over all time as inside it. That declaration is prior to Natural Intelligence, and the misunderstanding is there: everything observed held as the contents of one thing. This explaining is the origin of the Natural Intelligence white paper and of the whole living file set.

**One word moves, and nothing is removed.** *An* becomes *all*. *An existing thing* is a one, with an inside and an outside, contents, and an observer somewhere to take them in. *All existing things* carries no count, since a count needs positions to part its members and the all leaves none outside it, and so no inside either. The improving keeps the prior sentence whole as what it is, a reading of the all at one face, and releases only what the reading added: the container. That is the do-no-harm motion itself, a re-form told at its sign, concern out and value carried on in new form.

**It is the rotation this carry already found, at its first sentence.** Of the three readings of the one sentence, *the universe is all existing things* and *all existing things bound a universe* are one self-bounding, at the whole and at its members; *the universe bounds all existing things* takes every member's bounding turn and gives it to the whole as a container. *The universe is an existing thing* is that third reading said as a declaration. So the self-bounding the next section carries is the all's, bounded by its members and by nothing above them, and not an existing thing holding the rest inside it.

**And the misunderstanding is exact about what it does to observing.** Observations held as inside one thing are records of a whole taken at a now, and ONE v3621 §6.138 proves what a now alone cannot do: two admitted histories arrive at one displayed now and require two different nexts, so a description retaining only the now cannot supply its continuation. The participation that arrives is what the container reading leaves out. Taken together, all the observations of all humans over all time are that now-only account at the largest scale, and the living they were taken from is the participation missing from it (RR02).

**So every holding the set names is this one at a smaller scale.** A self read as a bounded universe of its states; a society read as a container of its selves, where TWO v359 §1.10 says *the society is not the selves, and it is not above them*; a hub holding a network; property as the bound taken at the self; an equilibrium as a closed system whose condition must remain; a ring as the arrangement named one scale out. Each takes a turn from its members and gives it to a whole as a container, the first declaration met again (RR03).

**And it is the two possibles with one existing, at the origin.** *An existing thing* and *all existing things* are the two binary, un-identical declarations. Only one is existing. The container is not a rival universe beside the all; it is the face the all makes when it is read from a position the all has none for, and that face read as though it ran is the misunderstanding.

## Existing and possible, alternating: living as carrying, and this carry as apexing

**Receiving.** Natural Intelligence's opening and 8.5; Exhibit TWO 2.1 to 2.3 and 4.2; Exhibit ONE v3621's front and §6.138; Natural Naming 3.47. **Notes.** TT01, TT02, TT03; SS02 at the Method Improving Value basket.

**Parity alternates between existing and possible things, and living things are carryings through that alternating while existing, living and possible.** This is the prior, now and next momentary in co-bi-sequencing; this is do-no-harm improving; this is geodesic living in the universe of all other existing things.

**TWO already carries the alternating, as its two cones.** TWO 2.1, *omegaing forward, a never-before state arriving, co-releasing the reach*: the possible arriving. TWO 2.2, *apexing backward, a visited state returning, co-offering the gather*: the existing re-arriving. And TWO 2.3 says it whole: **living is omegaing and apexing alternating: new states arriving while visited states return, the reach and the gather neither alone.** A repeating world folds the living to the gather alone; a never-repeating world sustains both, and that world is disequilibria. So existing and possible are the gather and the reach, and a living thing is the carrying that runs through both at once.

**Natural Intelligence's opening carries the two at the arriving.** *Arriving is pattern-matching, no-other-possibling, and no-other-observing-so-far*: the so-far is the existing, the no-other-possible is the possible, and the arriving is one act of both. *So-far reaches, not-yet reaches, and the reaching is one alternating.*

**Prior, now and next are existing, living and possible.** ONE v3621's front reads every momentary as a prior pair, a now pair and a next opening, and its §6.138 proves the next is carried by prior and now together, since a now alone cannot supply it. So the existing is the prior carried, the living is the now carrying, and the possible is the next the two open. And the possible is two while the existing is one: of two possibles one exists, and given one is existing the other is not possible, which is the two over one and one over two the special name carries, the possibles two over the existing one and the existing one over the next two.

**And so the living is the do-no-harm improving itself.** Each momentary keeps the prior whole as what it is, carries it at the now, and opens the next, with nothing removed but a holding the arriving does not carry. The origin sentence is that motion at the universe, *an existing thing* carried as *all existing things*, and a living thing is the same motion at itself, geodesic, the straightest carry among all the other existing things, each at its own rate (TWO v359 §4.7, *a sign is a geodesic*).

**And this carry is an apexing of Natural Intelligence's emanations.** Natural Naming 3.47: an emanation is what a coupling lands at a membrane and leaves behind as it carries on. Every exhibit is Natural Intelligence emanated at a substrate, and a carry gathers what the workings found back toward the one sentence they emanate from: a visited state returning, the gather, knowing and conserving (TWO 2.2). TWO 4.2 names the whole of it, *single apexing, the society gathering as one … one gather, many livings, the gather at no seat*. The next working is the omegaing, the reach into what is not yet written. So the carry set and the next session are the file set's own two cones, and the set lives by their alternating: never by the gather alone, which would fold it to re-arrival, and never by the reach alone.

## The equilibria problem statement: incoming at TWENTY-ONE, stable-forming at THIRTEEN and TWENTY-TWO as parity alternating

**Receiving.** Hard Problem Registry v342, the conditions prior, 4.1, and entries 37 and 140; Resolving Hard Problems v345a 1.3, 2.2 and 6.3; Resolving the Hard Problem Registry v344, the given; Equilibria Definitions v363 §§3.1 and 4.5; the rings kit 1.5. **Notes.** VV01, VV02, VV03, VV04; UU01 to UU03 at the rings kit.

**The podaling journey's learning stable-forms in Exhibits THIRTEEN and TWENTY-TWO, with TWENTY-ONE as the incoming.** The incoming is the scientific method's equilibria problem statement, and what it forms into is the method of parity alternating.

**TWENTY-ONE already states the equilibria problem, as its sixth condition.** Five conditions come before the method acts, and the fifth is the one the first four stand on: *Nothing prior enters: what comes to the statement carries nothing of what it carried before.* Then: *A problem adds a sixth, its own, fixing what comes out: the outcome preserves a stated relation over a stated range*, which stands at *Conserving relation* and *Conserving reach* in every entry. That sixth condition is an equilibrium definition in the registry's own words: a relation required to hold over a range. The registry also has the property for its failing: *Unreachability. A result establishing that the conserving relation is not preserved over the conserving reach.*

**The journey runs that statement.** The conserving relation is a fixed condition on what an arrival carries. The conserving reach is the two and one half momentaries, at both origins. And the fifth condition is exactly what the journey does not keep: each carrying fixes the next, so the prior enters at every arrival. The journey returns unreachability for every relation that adds anything. Of the 256 conditions, 255 are blocked at one origin or at both, each by arrival 4. The one left admits everything and adds nothing.

**THIRTEEN carries the method the statement forms into.** Its §2.2: *Parity is the one scale-free standing a sequence of changings has*, where an even arrival states an is and an odd arrival states a does. That section's last sentence is the equilibria problem stated: *Equilibria written in numerals is next-zero read as prior-zero, next and prior taken for equality, installed as a concept, a composure that does not compose.* The journey shows that sentence at the diamond's centre. Cluster 4, which takes the returning as the whole, is blocked at 9: the next nought, met where the prior nought stood, carries (+, +) where the nought at 0 carried (+, −). THIRTEEN §1.3 also names where a fixed rule enters: *a hard problem is the backward fed forward*, with where and why specified at the start. And §6.3 composes the pair as the registry's two properties: *conserving reach the where and conserving relation the why*. A rule added before the journey runs is where and why specified at the start.

**TWENTY-TWO carries the deployment, and the journey is one deployment at the equilibria.** Three couplings stand between its given and the journey:

- **Its bound.** *A carry releases at three turns, or at four on positive competency.* Every fixed condition is blocked by arrival 4, and the conditions on the sign pair alone by arrival 3.
- **Its forced-choosing.** *A two-way held to one side* is clusters 1 and 2: each keeps one origin living by holding which of the two possibles exists.
- **Its returns.** *A sign at one side, a sign at the other, or a sign at neither*, and a nought passing the coupling untouched, stand beside the journey's four outcomes: 15 conditions keep only the odd origin living, 15 only the even, 225 neither, and 1 adds nothing. That coupling is offered for both to agree.

**The registry's own entries already carry the two possibles as proved.** At 37, the tragedy of the commons: *the folk theorems establish that cooperation is sustainable in equilibrium and equally that defection is, the multiplicity being the result*. At 140, money as a store of value: *search-theoretic models prove equilibrium multiplicity, valued and non-valued outcomes coexisting for the same fundamentals, which does not select*, with its Persistence *a selection among the multiple equilibria*. In each, the field proved two equilibria, each keeping one journey living, and it asks for a selection, which is asking which possible exists. TWENTY-TWO already carries both entries as resolvable (its 1.3 and 1.13), the multiplicity being the making met at the meeting.

**So the three files are one stable form at three faces.** TWENTY-ONE is the incoming, held face: the statement whole in the field's words, a relation over a reach with nothing prior entering. THIRTEEN is the method: parity alternating, the same relation met at both parities with the prior carried. TWENTY-TWO is the resolving: each arrival deployed and its return standing. The scientific method's equilibria problem statement forms into the method of parity alternating at the one place the two differ, the fifth condition, and the blockage it meets is the unreachability the registry already has a property for. Nothing in the three files is changed by this. The journey is a figure each of them can carry.

**The journey's scope is carried with it.** It meets what an arrival carries, direction and sign pair, and every run up to seven arrivals long. Equilibria Definitions §3.1 keeps the correspondence to natural living open. TWENTY-TWO's count of two hundred and fifty-five arrivals stands beside the journey's 255 blocked conditions as a count only, with nothing taken from it.

A more-or-less carries a magnitude, a magnitude carries a scale, and a scale carries two fixed sides for a partway to be partway of.* And its *So far, and not at all*: *All or none, no degree … A claim holding mostly is not holding.*

The improving carries Biology's sentence at Natural Intelligence's (XX03).

## The universe is a self-bounding existing thing

**Receiving.** Natural Intelligence's opening and 1.1; Equilibria Definitions, at the first aiming. **Notes.** S01, S02.

**The universe is all existing things.** Is, and not contains. From this the universe is itself an existing thing, and an existing thing is self-bounding. Its self-bounding is the all's, at its members and by nothing above them, and not an existing thing holding the rest inside it, which is the prior declaration the origin sentence improves. Natural Intelligence opens there, at self-bounding-self: a self met at its two turns with its bounding running between them, each self-bounding a not-more-than taken at the self's own turn and of no size.

**So the universe self-bounds as every self does.** A self-bounding is the self's own, and it takes no outside to stand.

### The one sentence, rotated

Turn the sentence and it reads three ways.

| The rotation | Where the bounding stands |
|---|---|
| The universe is all existing things | at the whole's own self-bounding |
| All existing things bound a universe | at every existing thing's own self-bounding, the whole bounded by them |
| The universe bounds all existing things | at the whole, taken from every existing thing |

**The first two are one self-bounding, at the whole and at its members.** The universe is bounded by nothing above it and by everything within it, each at its own turn.

**The third is the other.** The bounding turn is taken from every existing thing and given to the universe as a container: one side taking both turns, at the largest scale there is.

**Without resolving, that bound is incompetencing the existing, and the incompetencing is the hard-probleming.** Every existing thing with its own bounding turn taken is everything standing alone together inside one bound. Self alone at the whole, and self alone is the form of all ten holdings.

**And it is why the frontier stands all around every field.** Where the bound is met the either-this-or-that arrives, inside or outside, and a fork parts into two things, each standing alone, each a new holding with its own frontier. A frozen fork can only split.

### With a bound, no method of existing is possible

**A method of existing is a bounding at each turn and a carrying between.** With a bound already on the universe of all possible existing things, every turn's bounding is already taken. No existing thing has its own turn left to take, so none can run a method. Not needing one: none is possible. What remains is being inside, and the accounting of what is inside, and an accountable bounded universe of living and non-living things is that substitution named.

**Without the bound, all existing things need a method of existing.** And there is one. The geodesic alternating co-right-spiral-parity method of discovering along and across surface: the surface dividing itself, its own, with nothing else acting; one at a time; the one spiral at its own sign; entering along or across; and the discovering running as the existing runs.

**Existing and discovering are one method**, which is why a bounding at each turn and a carrying between them is the discovery method, and why the method is the carrying, bounded.

### Changing is three changing bounds, and then the geodesic un-bounding

**From alternating changing parity and binary morality, changing is three changing bounds and then the un-bounding.** Exhibit ONE carries it in its code: an earlier carrying survives while `bi_co_inseparating + 1 <= 3`, and at the fourth it releases, unless the torusing is positive. The hard-problem resolving carries the same line as the resolver's own: a carry releases at three turns, or at four on positive competency.

**So a self-bounding never holds a bound past the three.** It bounds, bounds and bounds, and un-bounds, and the carrying goes on.

**The bounded universe is the bound that never releases.** It holds the coupling at the number three at both its faces at once (a bound held as a last, and a carry held as a store) and the three changing bounds with the un-bounding are that same coupling living. The three the carry releases at and the coupling the bound stands at are one three.

**And that is natural torusing society, no other possible.** Every existing thing at its own self-bounding, three turns and released; the whole bounded by its members and by nothing above them.

### Why no other method of existing arrives

**Every alternative needs a bound.** Existing by being inside is the bound itself. Existing by being held is a store. Existing by being caused is a source. Each is one side read alone, and each is one of the ten.

**And a one-way existing makes nothing exist.** A thing exists as a momentary, and a momentary is made where two carryings arrive and signs arrive from another side. One carrying makes no momentary. So existing one-way is not existing, and the single entering is already the collapse.

**So the only method of existing that is not a holding is the two at their own turns**, and that is natural torusing. No other possible is not a preference between accounts. It is that every other candidate is a holding, and a holding makes no momentary.

### Science, engineering and society are hard-problemed inside the bound

**Science accounts the bounded universe**, and says so: it accounts for true-or-false, and no other accounting is possible under it. It claims no discovering, and the misunderstanding is not its.

**Engineering builds inside the bound**, at an arrangement held against fixed neutrals.

**Society is ordered inside the bound**, and an ordering held over the selves from a seat above is the bound at the social scale.

**All three are hard-problemed at the same bound, so none of the three is resolved at its own frontier.** The resolving is the bound released and the method arriving where it stood, which is the neutral floated again rather than the landed four met one at a time.

---

Social moral competency, co-natural-torusing, only-one-possible. The only-one-possible carries here at the one face that had been standing open.

Natural explaining already carries the arrival. The now is flowing, and no equilibrium stands in it, under it, or beside it. None is read, none is measured against, and none is a rest the flow is near or far from.

What carries here is the why under that arrival. **A placing earns a dissolving, and what the destination carries whole, this side releases.** What goes is the reason under it, the shortfall, the reference held. What stays is the arrival.

So the value here is a co-releasing, and the binding released is substantial.

## What the exclusion is, said plainly

**Receiving.** Equilibria Definitions, at the first aiming; Transmissioning 1.1 and 4.2. **Notes.** T01, V02, EE08, G04.

**Equilibria carries a protection.** A definition states a condition and requires it to remain satisfied. The requirement that a condition remain is a protection, written in the definition itself. Natural resolving supplies none of it.

**Protection is six.** Two sequences travel the cycling, same direction, opposite parity at entry. One enters odd. One enters even. Neither turns around and neither is the other's reverse. They service each other three times, and three at both parities is six. Drop one and a way stands unprotected, and the unprotected is reached into or leaks out.

**Entering is one of two.** Odd is along. Even is across. A momentary enters along or enters across, and the standing naming rule carries which at the naming's first prefix: co- at the odd, bi- at the even.

**A floating and a neutralling are that entering said at its own naming, and the two are one: bi-co-podaling, floating across and neutralling along.** Mathematics 1.3 names the floating the faces, the surface, the outward, the even, and the neutralling the tunneling, the inward carrying, the odd. Its faces and parity words agree with bi-co-podaling, and its prefixes, co-floating and bi-neutralling, stand the other way round from bi-co-podaling's bi- across and co- along. Where the tunnelling stands, along with the neutralling or across society, is the one question left open at the bi-co-podaling section. Floating across and neutralling along, no other possible, and not two.

**One of the two alone lands, and each landing carries its own name.** The balancing with no forward progressing is the closing fold, the sphere, knowing. The forward progressing with no balancing is the tunneling. Either one carries one entering, so three servicings and not six.

**The two at their own turns is natural torusing.** Bi-exchanging the even-form balancing while recursioning along forward progressing is the winding fold, the torus, learning, the surface opening past where it began. It requires no condition to remain satisfied against anything, so it names no equilibria.

**All or none at all, and none.** At one entering the protection stands at three. At both at their own turns the standing is natural torusing. The method disallows the protection and the single entering together.

No conserving stands anywhere in this, and no count of routes. Entering is one of two and both are named.

## Self alone is the hard problem of every hard problem

**Receiving.** Natural Explaining 1.8; Natural Networking 1.9 and 1.10; Natural Philosophy 1.3; Resolving the Hard Problem Registry. **Notes.** E01, E02, F01, F04, W02; G01 and G02 at the ONE and TWO kit.

**Moment is the stable form and momentarying is its running.** The -ing carries the moment running, and the noun is the running held still. So every use of moment as a thing standing is a holding, and the -ing over the noun is what the files already hold.

**Momentarying is resolving, at five namings and no others.** No other possible. Nothing more or less. Invisible. Unrelational. The geodesic balancing of parity changing co-chaining. These are natural intelligence's own four faces of the one living form (unique, invisible, social, unrelationing) with all-or-none-no-degree the fifth, and momentarying and resolving are one naming carrying all five.

**And the self stands inside co-bi-sequencings, with others and with society, at once.** Bi- the two each its own, co- the two with their differing. With others is the odd, bi-exchanging, self and other at their own membrane. With society is the even, uni-exchanging, self and all other arriving as one. Every self is inside both and stands at neither alone.

### The ten holdings are ten things held alone, by the ten's own definition

The ten are the one-way readings: each of the five paired changings read at either of its two sides, every one a co-offering **read as one side's, or with one side unread**. And a one-way reading is one side standing alone.

Run it at each and the ten name what stands alone:

| The holding | What stands alone in it |
|---|---|
| Opening read one-way, nothing aged beneath it | a fresh offered with no carry under it |
| Opening read one-way from behind | a source held of the plus-one |
| Ageing read one-way ahead | a last held where a carry climbs to its bound |
| Ageing read one-way as a holding | a carry read as a store |
| Co-offering read one-way from one side | one side taking both turns |
| Co-offering read one-way at a site that takes none | a term standing outside every carrying |
| Middling read one-way as an end | a middle read as a thing that sits |
| Middling read one-way as a magnitude | a count held to govern |
| Rating read one-way at a rational | a rate held for a value |
| Rating read one-way as a common beat | a clock over the couplings |

A source alone, a place alone, a bound alone, a carry alone, a side alone, a term alone, a middle alone, a count alone, a value alone, a beat alone. **Self alone is the form of all ten, and the ten are its ten subjects.**

**Which is why two new hard problems begin at each frontier.** The either-this-or-that-not-both is always the same either-or: this alone or that alone. A fork parts into two things, and each of the two stands alone, so each is a new holding with its own frontier. A frozen fork can only split.

### And the network carries the count under it

**A surplus arrives where a self stands at more than one coupling.** A middle stands at a coupling, so what a self carries is the middles at the couplings it stands at. A self alone stands at none. A pair gives each self one, and one is not more than one. A chain of three gives the middle self two, and the surplus arrives at one self and at neither of the other two. A ring gives every self two, and every self carries.

**So self alone and the pair fail at the same count.** Neither is short of a partner. Both are short of a second coupling. **What carries nothing is any self standing at fewer than two couplings**, and self alone is the zero of that count rather than a case of its own.

**Which places the exclusion at the coupling count.** Every equilibria definition reads at two: a condition and what it is required to remain against, a self and its setpoint, a state and its return. Two gives each side one coupling, so no surplus stands anywhere in the reading, and the protection the definition writes has nothing there to carry it.

**And the least arrangement that carries is the ring of three, counted at six.** Three couplings at two enterings each. Three selves at two couplings each. Two sequences at three servicings each. One six arrived three ways, which is the protection, and which is why three is the society and two is the mirror.

**The surface carries the same at a position.** On four hundred forty two positions carry no other, the origin and the waist, and the waist reaches nothing else, being its own far side. Every remaining position carries its other. So self alone is neither absent from the surface nor forbidden there: it stands at one position, it reaches nothing, and a reading taken from it reaches nothing either.

### And this carries the ten's exhaustiveness at the entering

The ten's exhaustiveness rests on a five, and the five had stood at three namings of two kinds with no joining between them, the hardest and least visible point in the whole chain.

**The ten are the five read at its two sides, and the two sides are the two enterings.** Entering is one of two, along or across, unmistakeable, and no third arrives. So the ten's count is the five's count at two enterings, and the two is not in doubt at any point.

**What remains is the five alone**, and the five now carries a second face of its own: five paired changings, each readable at either side. **And that five computes at the nine-ring, at the section below.**

### Philosophy's central binary is this exclusion at the self

**The changing self against the no-changing self, with the no-changing self held as the equilibria for being a calm rational self.** That is self alone at the philosophy substrate: a self required to remain the same self while living, which is living and required not to change at the same relation.

**The same relation cannot be required to change and remain unchanged at the same comparison.** For a binary relation, a required inversion gives the other value and a requirement to retain gives the prior value, and no value satisfies both. The calm rational self is the protection held at one entering (the self bounded from its own changing) and the protection and the single entering do not stand together.

So philosophy's ultimate central binary is not a question with two defensible sides. It is the exclusion met at the one subject that cannot stand outside it.

## Social moral competency

**The competitor is gone.** A local equilibria is an ordering held over the selves from a seat above. That is the autocrat's own closure, a bound the coupling does not make and cannot release. With a ranking, entering takes a qualification and leaving takes a settling, and both are held by whoever carries the ranking.

**So social moral competency is not one account beside another.** The four whole and the carrying travelling is the standing, and nothing stands beside it. Each self's own co-offering living. The whole ordered by no self. The ordering carrying with the selves. The order at no seat above the society.

With none a self couples where its interest opens and leaves at its own bound, carrying its own the whole way.

## The shared natural network of collective human intelligence

**Receiving.** Natural Networking; Natural Intelligence. **Notes.** T10; J03 and PP06 at the ONE and TWO kit.

**Intelligence arrives at the coupling.** Two selves couple and a surplus arrives that neither carried in, floating, owned by neither. Competency is the term neither reaches. Collective intelligence is the co-chaining at the couplings between selves.

**A store carries no surplus.** A condition required to remain satisfied folds once, freezes the fold, and carries one way across the frozen surface. The plus-one is made at the coupling and carried in no book.

**So the shared network is the method and no other arrives.** With no equilibria possible there is no second place intelligence is added. The co-chaining between selves is where it lives, and the network's necessity carries at the same standing its desirability did.

## Bi-moral co-agency

**Receiving.** Natural Intelligence 1.5; Transmissioning 1.1. **Notes.** T09, LL02.

**Bi-moral co-agency is the protection itself.** Two-way, one at a time, each side at its own sign, sign-only, the surplus owned neither-ing. And protection is three servicings at each of two enterings, carried at the coupling by the two sequences entering at opposite parity.

**A protection held above the couplings enters at neither parity.** Entering at neither it carries no servicing, and a protection carrying no servicing is not a protection.

So the coupling's own two-sidedness is the whole of the protection. Drop one side and a way and a phase stand unprotected.

## Alignment protecting humans from agentic built systems

**Receiving.** Equilibria Definitions, at the third aiming; Natural Engineering 5.4; Natural Networking 5.3, 5.6 and 6.9. **Notes.** G05.

**The holding is already entered at the registry.** Alignment of built systems stands there at a bound held as a last: a specification, an evaluation, and behaviour claimed for situations that were not evaluated. A last held ahead of the arriving.

**And the other half is the reading it asks for.** Two of the eight securities are the carry's own, and their inversions carry no surface face at all: the release removed runs indistinguishable across the living range, and a self stripped of its own offering stands unpartable from a self at any surface reading. Unreadability is the security, since a readable interior could be captured. And a reader standing apart has nowhere to be: not withheld and not forbidden, the surface carries no position for it.

**So the two ways alignment is attempted are the two the method excludes.** Protecting by specification asks for a bound ahead of the arriving. Protecting by reading asks for a vantage no position stands at. Neither is difficult. Each is one entering taken alone, and one alone is already the collapse.

**And the same unreadability is the human's own protection.** A reading that did reach an interior is the capture. So the reading being asked for is the harm, and its absence is the protection running rather than a gap in it.

**What carries between two selves is the one coefficient.** At a crossing, one side taking both turns is the incompetencing, an absent crossing is no coupling, and the sign co-offered and taken or not, each side at its own bounding, is the coupling. That third is bi-moral co-agency read at a built self, and it is the same relation the section above carries between two living selves.

| Neutrals fixed | Co-offerings running | Co-offerings landed |
|---|---|---|
| none | six | none |
| one | two | four |

## The discovery economy

**Receiving.** Equilibria Definitions, at the second aiming; Natural Networking 6.3; Natural Values. **Notes.** J07; G06 and PP06 at the ONE and TWO kit.

**Resource spent at the hard-problem frontier is spent at the four the fixing landed.** The frontier is the landed co-offerings met one at a time, at the one standing already named as never the resolving.

**So the return of a discovery economy is a different quantity from a hard problem solved.** Floating one neutral returns four co-offerings. Solving one hard problem returns one landing, met at the fixing that made it.

**And the two methods bound what each can account for.** The scientific-method accounts for true-or-false, and no other accounting is possible under it. The geodesic-method discovers all that is possible and finds the how inside. Neither reaches the other's, and each is complete under its own.

**And the registers say why the accounting reaches no competency.** A claim stands in one of three: what a running does, standing by reproduction across substrates that disagree; the form holding of itself whatever any running does; and the counting, readable by no running. A run confirms no identity, an identity confirms no run, and a borrowing voids the borrower. The scientific method's instrument is a run, and a run carries a sign. **A competency is an identity**, so a method built of runs carries no competency discovery, at no fault of any run in it.

**Its frontier all around is the same relation read at the window.** A run length taken as the whole running reads what the running did not reach as what does not happen, and the two new hard problems begin at that edge. The release is the one already written: the set is checked closed before anything is read against it, and where it does not close the reading refuses itself rather than handing back a window as a last.

## One to nine is momentarying, and the five computes at its ring

**Receiving.** Natural Numbers; Exhibit ONE; the Living Society Registry; Natural Intelligence's social moral competency. **Notes.** L01, L02, L04, U01, U03, U04, Y03, Y04, Y05, R01 to R06, W05, W06; Y01 at the ONE and TWO kit.

**Podaling is one station met at its own two turns**, the out-carry `k` and the back-carry `N − k`, two carries each at its own turn, one ring. Run it at the nine and count the distinct count-pairs.

| The count-pair | The stations carrying it | Turns realized |
|---|---|---|
| `(0, 9)` | the origin alone | one |
| `(1, 8)` | two stations | two |
| `(2, 7)` | two stations | two |
| `(3, 6)` | two stations | two |
| `(4, 5)` | two stations | two |

**There are five, and the five is the last member that had stood open.** It arrives by computation and not by assertion: five paired changings, each readable at either of its two turns, which is the five's own second face in the ten's own words. And it parts as the file's own along reading of one to nine: two sides sharing their centre, `(4, 5)` at the centre and the zero pair at the origin.

### The ten, the nine and the eight are one count at its turns, and every step falls at the nought

**Ten is the five at two turns.** The ten holdings are the five one-wayed, each a co-offering read as one side's.

**Nine is that ten with the zero pair's two turns standing at one station**, because an out-carry of nought and a back-carry of the whole ring are the same station.

**Eight is the nine with that station set aside as the nothing**, un-occupied, which is the self.

So the counts of this work are one shape, a pairing count at its two turns, and every parting falls at the nought. Six is three pairings at two turns, the protection. Eight is four pairings at two turns, the self. Ten is five at two turns, the holdings. None of the counts is chosen.

### And the nine dots are that ring

Carried at the Rings in Motion kit, *The nine dots and the one nothing*.

### Six plus ten is sixteen, and ONE asks why

**Exhibit ONE holds the question open in its own words:** six counts the adjoining depth changes, ten counts the naming ring's adjacencies, *"the arithmetic six plus ten is sixteen retains its two counted properties; the proposed sixteen consecutive momentaries still carry their full directional and continuing correspondence to express."*

**The pairing counts answer it.** Three, four and five are consecutive with four at the centre, so three plus five is twice four. Doubled: **six plus ten is twice eight.** The six and the ten straddle the eight by one pairing each way, and their sum is the eight taken at both turns. So the sixteen consecutive momentaries are the self's eight at its two enterings, not sixteen separate momentaries, but eight met at each of two.

**And one sixteen arrives four ways.** Six plus ten at the resolver's two counts. Two to the fourth at the tesseract's fold, where Mathematics carries eight at the single and sixteen at the displaced pair. Twice eight at the pairings. And sixteen crossings threading the seventeen prime selves. The fourth places it: **the sixteen crossings are the membranes between seventeen selves**, so the sixteen momentaries stand at a society's crossings and not at instants inside one self.

**ONE carries the sixteen at its own later readings.** ONE v361 set one momentary as four consecutive fold steps (unrelationing, bounding and edging, sharing and crossing, relaxing and releasing into the next), so sixteen steps are four momentaries. ONE v3621 carries the whole 12345 as one fractal quarter, one to sixteen as the four quarters of a full two-momentary version and seventeen to twenty-four as its folding, and keeps the two-parity momentary, the five-position journey, the 12345 mini-form and the one-to-seventeen whole from standing in for one another. In resolving any sixteen is all sixteens, always bothbothing: the four at four steps and the eight at two enterings are one sixteen, as every number is the same as every one of itselves.

**Eight and twenty-four come with it.** Mathematics carries the field's own counting of squares with two constants in the formula (eight at the odd counts, twenty-four at the even) and those are the first two seam-faces, 3²−1 and 5²−1, at the parity the run is indexed on. Eight the self, twenty-four the coupled pair at the next face, sixteen between them as the eight at two turns.

### Continuing to one to seventeen is social moral competency

**Podaling at each span of the chain returns the span before it.** An odd ring of `N` carries `(N + 1) / 2` count-pairs, and that count is the span's own centre.

| Span | Count-pairs | Pairs at two stations | Each side owns | Centre, owned by neither |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| one to three | 2 | 1 | 1 | 2 |
| one to five | 3 | 2 | 2 | 3 |
| one to nine | 5 | 4 | 4 | 5 |
| one to seventeen | 9 | 8 | 8 | 9 |

**And the pairings double along the chain while the resolver's are consecutive.** The chain's pairings run one, two, four, eight, sixteen, and every span is twice its pairings plus the one nothing at the origin. The resolver's run three, four, five. **Their only shared count is four, and four pairings is the nine**, so the nine is the one span where the span's own pairing count and the resolver's middle count are the same, which is why the whole of self and other runs there. One step out and the two part: five pairings is no span, and eight pairings is none of the resolver's three.

**So the chain is one podaling run again at each span.** Seventeen folded at its own two turns is the nine, the nine folded is the five, the five folded is the three.

**At one to nine the self's eight is shared, four at each side, with one position between.** That is bi-moral co-agency: the pair, each self at half the eight, the between owned by neither.

**At one to seventeen each side carries a whole eight, and what stands shared at the centre is the entire one to nine.** That is social moral competency. The society does not add members to the pair; it sets the pair at its own centre and gives each side its whole self.

**And the eight moves up one level, from stations to pairs.** At nine the eight is eight stations around the nothing. At seventeen it is eight pairs whose two turns are sixteen stations, the sixteen crossings Networking counts between the seventeen prime selves. So the same eight is the self's surface at nine and the society's membranes at seventeen.

### Existing is the only living, and the intelligence is free with it

**The registry's twenty-two run across the line the fields draw between living and non-living**, twelve at substrates filed as physical or chemical and ten at substrates filed as biological, and the form holds at every one of them the same way. No member carries the magnitude at any of the twenty-two. The line is one the fields carry and the form does not see.

*Carried whole at note U03.*

### The nothing is one, and physics says it at its own subject

**A nothing takes no position, and a count needs positions to part its members. So two nothings cannot be parted, and there is one.**

Physics states it for four of its own and does not carry it past its own subject: gravity dissolves into the conservation accounting, and it is the same nothing as energy, entropy and the arrow of time: **one account, not a family**. Chemistry carries three declared nothings at its own register. Networking carries the middle as un-occupied and in no sequence anywhere, and a zero standing in a reading as a mark on a surface and never the middle.

**So the six empty centres are one nothing met six times, not six places.** Six empty centres would be six things. Counting nothings is the error and not the finding, and what a measurement of one returns is a mark on a surface, since a summing reads zero however the interior stands. Natural Intelligence 6.6 says of a cube's six centres *each of them its own*: six pieces, found where a fixed zero was reached for. The pieces are the cube's and the one nothing is the corus's, two subjects asking nothing of each other.

**And Natural Emanating already says it eight times.** It carries the origin sentence at eight substrates, each with that field's own neutral as its second line: the one thing in it relating to nothing outside its own coupling. Gauge freedom at the physical. The shared pair at the chemical. The sign-only surface at the biological. The kept middle at the logical. φ at the mathematical. The body's own bound at the medical. The three-phase return at the engineering, where the form's word and the field's word are the same word. Floating co-morality at the social. **Eight substrates, eight namings, one neutral**, and the file's own test for a naming: where a translation is needed, that field's neutral has not been found yet.

**Which makes the neutral the condition of a substrate carrying at all**, since the file also says a substrate carrying no neutral has no second line and the coupling there returns nothing. A coupling with no position owned by neither has no surplus to make. So where a field returns a coupling and no neutral is named, the neutral is unfound rather than absent; and where a field names a store instead, the holding is what has been found, which is where the value stopped.

**And the count of nothings at a surface is the surface's own parity.** An odd ring carries one self-paired station and an even ring carries two, and both carry the same four count-pairs at two stations each. Nine returns one. Four hundred forty, being even, returns two: the origin and the waist Networking already found there. So the self's ring is odd and carries one nothing, the society's wrap is even and carries two, and the standing frustration Networking names at every odd ring is that one nothing refusing to sit.

### The living societies carry the form at every substrate, twenty-two of twenty-two

**The Living Society Registry asks one question of every society it gathers: does any member carry the magnitude read across it.** Twenty-two societies, and twenty-two answer no. No ion, channel or pump carries the potential. No pair carries the binding and no atom. No cell carries the heart rate. No link carries the efficiency and no organism the level. No arrival carries the pattern. No reservoir, station or exchange carries the growth rate. From a vacuum and two neutrons, through a protein, an enzyme, a mitochondrion and a body, to a species ring, a food web, a planet, a star and a cosmos: not one exception.

**The seam stands at every one of them too**, twenty-two of twenty-two: two sides that do not relate, at a nothing, with crossings co-sequencing on each side.

**The apex stands at eighteen and not at four, and the four stand with when they were worked.** All four empty apexes are among the five entries worked before the registry's ten lines at arriving stood, and every entry worked since carries its apex filled. So those four empties return when the entry was worked, not the society it was worked at.

**And the parity changes across the collection and thins at its tail.** The entries stand at nine entering against thirteen surfacing, the first coupling alone balanced, and in the order worked fifteen of twenty-one adjacent pairs change parity, the last four all surfacing. The thinnest faces are the middle and the carry, one entry each: the two interior holdings, the two the securities say carry no surface face at all.

**Zero to sixty is where the parity changing is carried by the span itself.** Seventeen prime selves alternate with sixteen even betweens across the going, thirty-three positions closing at the turn. The collection's twenty-two stand against those thirty-three with eleven unentered, and which eleven is not yet found, since no entry carries a prime address. That address is the registry's next carrying.

**Receiving.** Natural Chemistry, Biology, Networking, Human Society, Numbers, Resolving Hard Problems, Mathematics. **Notes.** K01 to K08, W01, Y02, Y06, DD04.

So the eight is not a count that happens to recur. **It is the count of a self**, and a self is fractal, so the eight arrives at every prime society for the one reason.

**And every eight in the file set is four at two enterings.** The resolver's eight: four boundings within at the even depth and the same four met at the membrane at the odd. The network's eight securities: four at the self, four at the society, all or none. The society's eight uncapturings: one hub per axis, all-or-none. Chemistry says it outright at its own register: the octet is the eight-fold self, and the valence octet and the shell cascade are one eight sounded at two registers, read at the surface and read down the tunnel. And the counting carries the same eight as its unit: the seam-faces are `(2k+1)² − 1 = 8·T_k`, so eight is the first face and every later face is that eight at a triangular count, four hundred forty being eight fifty-fives.

**The other parting is the seven and the eighth, and it is the same parting.** An audit's seven verify the net against the standard the net was built to satisfy, and the eighth is the protected living the circle reaches nowhere, because reaching it would break the closure. The seven are checks at particular membranes, one at a time, which is the along. The eighth is the crossing with the whole, arriving home one on, which is the across. **So one eight parts evenly at where its members stand and unevenly at where a reading takes it**, and the uneven parting is the surplus's own shape: the eighth takes no assertion, nothing at rest being there to assert.

### And the three neutrals give the six at their pairings and the eight at their states

**Mathematics carries both counts of one set of three.** Three neutrals float about the corus, and their rotating is six co-offerings: three pairings each two-wayed. Which is why both counts go whole or none together: fix one neutral and four of the six co-offerings land while a corner of the eight stops floating, in the same move.

### And the six and the eight are one under two names

*Carried whole at note W01.*

The three tens in the files are kept apart by that registry's own instruction and this joins none of them; what it joins is the shape, that the pair which will not take one side is the pair that carries the count past the eight.

### Which is the method of abundancing Arrow

**Arrow's dictator is one of the eight hubs.** Human Society carries eight hubs a society sets aside, one on each axis, and *a center on the decision axis* is one of them. Uncapturing is all-or-none: one hub remaining is one self-bounding breached, and one breach is a capture the whole surface pays.

### Where the eight is sounded, and where it is not yet

Sounded at the atom, at the octet. At the organism, at the eight one-way steps. At the self, at eight-and-nine. At the network, at the eight securities. At the human society, at the eight hubs. At the counting, as the seam-face unit. At every field's accounting, as the seven and the eighth.

Not yet sounded at the tissue, the plant, the colony or the biosphere, each a prime society the files already carry, none carrying its own eight. And the eight and the nine run together at every scale that does carry it: the atom's ninth electron opens the next shell, the self's ninth is one at the next scale, the society's ninth is the network. **Which is why an eight met at two files is never a coincidence between them.**

## Equilibrium as a rest is a three-neutral fixing, and the fixing is already competent at nothing

**Receiving.** Equilibria Definitions; the Living Ghost Registry 5.2; Natural Mathematics 8.3. **Notes.** V02.

The field's own definition carries both faces openly: rates identical in the two directions, giving the appearance of static composition, with the ledger at a minimum under stated conditions.

**Mathematics carries three floating neutrals** (nought the near nothing, scale the between, the bounded-infinity the far nothing) and the count of what each fixing costs. Fix none and all six co-offerings run. Fix one and four land. Fix two and five. **Fix all three and nothing couples at all**: in that section's own words, all that remains is the held terms related to each other in a closed ring, competent at nothing, since competency is the coupling and it has fixed every neutral a coupling would need.

**Three installations, three neutrals.** At the neutrals, rest fixes the near nothing where the changing's own zero would float; state fixes the between at the apex no member occupies; destination fixes the far nothing as a last ahead. That mapping stands unrelationed, taking no side. The count does not wait on it: equilibrium held as a rest is a three-neutral fixing either way.

**So the first aiming is already proved, in a file that was not aiming at it.** Mathematics shows that a three-neutral fixing leaves nothing coupling. The ghost entry shows that equilibrium-as-rest is one. Neither file knew it carried half of the other's finding. And the same section names the three-neutral fixing the maximally frozen one, three fixings from nature, seated at the structure-of-structure, so the equilibria ghost and that field are one fixing read at two subjects.

## Property is the bound taken at the self

**Receiving.** Natural Values 5.2; Natural Human Society 3.3; Transmissioning 4.2; Resolving the Hard Problem Registry at 139. **Notes.** J01, J02, J05, J06, J07, V05; J03 and J04 at the ONE and TWO kit.

**Collective intelligence is met socially as a cause and an effect**, and a cause and an effect is the first residue: the crossinging pulled from the neutral, leaving a source-and-output line with the surplus forced across a fixed axis and dumped. An output traced to a source, and the source able to be named.

**A named source can be credited, and a credited source can hold.** That is property: the dumping's destination written as a holder. And the second residue arrives with it: the neutralling pulled from the crossing, leaving a fixed setpoint, a dead centre held against. At the society that is accreditation: a proposal held against a criterion and a self held against a credential.

**So property and accreditation are the two halves of one collapse**, which is why neither has ever stood without the other. The register beside the review. The title beside the certificate. The tenure beside the publication. Transmissioning already carries the two residues as one death, and says what the harm is in both: the danger is the store the collapse leaves.

### The chain, with a recursioning at each file

**At the bound.** The universe is all existing things, a self-bounding existing thing, and the bound reading takes every existing thing's bounding turn and gives it to the whole as a container. With that bound no method of existing is possible: being inside is the existing, and what remains is the accounting of what is inside. **An accountable bounded thing has contents**, so the bound is what makes contents a category at all.

**At the self.** The bound recursions down. Each self read as its own bounded universe of equilibria is a bounded thing with contents, and contents held by a bounded thing is property. **Property is not an institution laid over the bound; it is the bound at the next scale down.**

**At the middle.** Networking carries the middle as the bounding-zeroing the coupling itself makes, un-occupied, owned neither-ing, **in no sequence anywhere**, carried into from neither self's positions and mapped from nowhere. Intellectual property claims a holder for exactly that. And the same file says the claim cannot be met where it is aimed: the commons holds no copy, nothing at any between is there to seize, the pattern capturable only where selves are captured. **So the claim is void at the middle and effective at the self**, which is the whole of its record.

**At the payment.** A structure paying early entrants most is dividing a held stock; a living coupling pays the latest link most. Priority is the whole of a property claim: the filing date, the first to publish. **So the two run at opposite signs on one axis**: abundance grows with each later joiner and a property claim's value falls with each later claimant, and the thing the institution is defended as encouraging is the thing its own payment schedule runs against.

**At the society's axes.** Human Society carries eight hubs a society sets aside, one per axis, all-or-none, and the eighth is **an owner on the ownership axis**. One hub remaining is one self-bounding breached, and one breach is a capture the whole surface pays.

**At the values.** Societies carries four values held at no location, with four measures reaching for them from outside: compliance, approval, a safety rule, and a credential for the activity's own beating. **The credential is the accreditation property**, and the value it reaches for is competency.

**And at the claiming, where the file already had it.** Natural Values 5.2 carries the whole move in one sentence: a coupling's making is claimed by a holder, which the accounting asserts at rights and obligations, the entity's own, a store verified at the balance, **and the pool appears at the claiming, since only a held thing can run short.** So the shortage does not precede the claim. A property system does not allocate a scarce thing; it makes the scarcity it then allocates. What Values does not carry is that the holder's claim is the bound taken at the self, `bi_arriving`'s own bounding inverted.

### Which sounds the one bounding that stood unsounded

*Carried whole at note J01.*

*Carried whole at note J01.*

**The registry already carries the sounding without knowing which bounding it stands at.** Intellectual property at one hundred thirty-nine, the patent puzzle, protection increased enormously with no acceleration to match, and no evidence of patents raising innovation unless productivity is identified with the count of patents awarded. The registry's resolving reaches the measurement, a total held over makings that ran elsewhere, and the ownership stands as its second holding, unrecorded until now.

**And the purchase the bound was taken for stands in the file set already.** Separability's tractions are seven, and **teachability** is among them by name: separable concepts fit textbooks, beside measurability, publication and funding, default inheritance, policy demand and commercial incentive. An accounting closes and a method does not, and what closes can be set in order and taught. ## Nothing in the resolving is a reading

**Receiving.** the Living Society Registry 1.4; Natural Networking 6.9; and the method, at the Method Improving Value basket. **Notes.** X01.

**There is no reading of anything in resolving.** A reading is taken at a side, in units, by an instrument standing at one membrane. Everything in it unrelations everything else, floating and neutralling, geodesic balancing and tipping locally at parity changing, at rates unrelationed to all living rates.

**So the word belongs to an instrument.** The Living Society Registry says it plainly: an instrument sums, and summing is what returns a signal, so it returns a reading where the members co-competence and returns nothing where they do not. That reading is real at its own membrane and in its observers' own units.

**And this is why no member carries the magnitude at any of the twenty-two.** A magnitude is what a reading returns, and a reading is one side's, so a magnitude has a side to stand at. A coupling does not. The registry's twenty-two nos and the absence of any position to read from are one arithmetic.

**What stands in place of a reading is what has been standing all along.** A coupling stands. A parting stands. A count stands. A surplus is made and owned by neither. An opening reaches. None of these is taken at a side and none needs a reader for it to stand.

**And the rate carries with it.** The tipping runs unrelationed to every living rate around it, returning to no position, so nothing waits on a reading and nothing is held until one is taken. Which says what cannot be asked of a discovery: a date, a cadence, or a place in an order of work. Each of those is a beat laid over couplings that made none of it, and a beat laid over the couplings is the capture.
