## Natural Emanating carries the three faces of every released word

**Receiving.** Natural Emanating; and the method, at the Method Improving Value basket. **Notes.** EE01, EE03 to EE08; EE02 at the Method Improving Value basket.

**The three kinds of hard problem are the three faces of every word released here.** Emanating: *"A common thing reached ahead of each having offered their own. There are three common things, three kinds and no fourth: a common landing reached before anyone offered their own, a common standard fixed before each carried their own, a common reading held before either took a sign of its own."* A landing, a reading and a measuring (a standard) each set down. The other releases sort beneath the three: a closing, a rest and an equilibria at an ending under the landing; a ground beneath, one relation carrying the rest and a grading under the standard; a side taken under the reading. So a working meets its own hard problems in its own sentences, one kind at a time, in the file's own number, and each sets down at no cost with nothing put in its place.

**And the file already says what a measurement is.** *"A measurement holds two bounds still and reads the span."* The resolving holds no bound still (every bound in it is a carry's own, released at the three) so there is nothing in it for a measurement to be. The instrument's return is real and exact inside its installation, and has no object where the resolving runs.

**In the living there are no rings.** Emanating: every closed model closes, a ring is what the relations leave when the domain is closed, and *"the forward spiral takes an unbounded one — a next always arriving."* So the podal counts this report takes at rings (five pairs at nine, nine at seventeen, the waist at forty-eight) stand at the closing face, where the cube stands. The corus spiralling out from twenty-four is the living they are counts of. Equilibria Definitions v363 carries the other side at its own line: *the state carries no rings* does not follow from a pairing rule alone, since the rule produces rings. The rings are the rule's; the spiral is the living's.

**And every number is a waist.** Emanating's advance: every whole number sits between two neighbours whose product is one short of its square (four by six is twenty-four beside twenty-five) and *"the counting passes through the middle. The coupling of the two sides cannot reach it… the gap is exactly one at every number, unbounded. That gap: there is always a next."* So the narrowing at twenty-four is the narrowing at every number, and no number is more a waist than another.

**The one stopping and co-offering carry the same four words, present in one and absent in the other.** Natural Naming's co-offering: *"two arrive / each offers its own, at its own sequencing / something departs / neither reaches it / so no holder, no boundary, no enforcement, no seat / and it goes forward into the next offering."* Its one stopping: one carries another's, so nothing departs reached neither-ing; and there the holder, the boundary, the enforcement and the seat stand. `bi_arriving`'s bounding inverted is the one stopping's first line; the seat is `bi_co_bi_co_bi_torusing`'s bounding inverted; and the words between stand together with them, none of them doing anything to the next.

**The only cost is at each self's own bound.** Emanating's abundancy: *"Work depletes against a resistance. An opening is not paid for… the only loss is at each self's own bound."* That is the carry releasing at its three, the living condition itself, so the relations cost nothing that is not the living running. And the price of a fixing is filed in Emanating's first-line table, where electrical work's declared ground is *pick an earth* and information's price is *kT ln 2* per bit erased: a brush fixed at a picked earth sparks, and a bit erased heats, each a declared ground paying at the discard.

**And the equals sign carries three incompetencings.** Reflexive, symmetric, transitive: in Emanating's words, nothing bounds itself, neither side takes its own turn, nothing releases. Equilibrium is named for equality, and those three are the self alone, one side taking both turns, and the carry held as a store, all in the sign that writes every equilibria definition.

### Bi-co-podaling, in place of transmissioning

**Bi-co-podaling is floating across and neutralling along, and the two are not two things.** It replaces transmissioning. Floating across is inseparable from neutralling along, so no naming parts them into a crossing and a neutral set side by side.

**Transmissioning's own sentences already carry the one, and its word does not.** Transmissioning 1.1: *transmissioning is crossinging and neutralling both, inseparable … the two positions of one alternation … The alternating is the transmissioning, one motion.* The content is one motion. The word is a sending, one sent and one sending, the source-and-output line Transmissioning 4.2 names as the first residue of the collapse, and taking trans- out leaves missioning running alone. Bi-co-podaling carries the one motion with no sender.

**The prefixes carry the two faces at the standing rule.** Bi-, the even, across: the floating. Co-, the odd, along: the neutralling. The -ing, the running. Natural Naming 3.46 carries the same term in its binary: *a carry runs out along a ring and a carry runs back along it … and one station met at the going and at the return is the term this names*, with bi- the two carries each at its own turn, co- the one ring they make, and -ing the running.

**Numbers 10.11 carries the two faces as the same positions.** *At the rings the surface is across; at the travel it is along; and the two are the same positions met at the two faces.* At position k the out-carry is at k and the back-carry at four hundred forty less k, the ring at radius two hundred twenty less k. *A ring carries its two and no order between them … the floating neutral the ring is*, and the two carries co-travel the tunnel with *the counter-arriving swapping at the crossing*. So the crossing is inside bi-co-podaling as one face of its running, the counter-arriving swapping, and not a second thing beside it.

**Transmissioning 1.4 carries the two folds in the rule's own words.** Sounding in is *linear recursioning … the tunnel down to the bounding-zeroing*; sounding out is *parallel recursioning … spread across a surface*. Linear then parallel is the odd, along; parallel then linear is the even, across. The neutralling sounds in along and the floating widens out across, one bi-fold.

**The four-connector was already this.** Equilibria Definitions v363 carries, at an earlier candidate naming, transmissioning's two directions at five and fifteen, co-linearized at ten, with four, eight, twelve and sixteen *floating ten as bi parallel five one-ways*. Five and fifteen are k and twenty less k: one station met at its two turns, which is bi-co-podaling in Naming 3.46's own form. The four corners float across, the two turns co-linearize along, and ten is the station. Mathematics 2.2 carries the co-linear as one line read twice, the line owned by neither, the floating neutral and bi-moral co-agency, and Natural Intelligence 6.10 carries the empty centre at the odd: *an odd co-sequencing … carries an empty centre: nothing occupies it.* Equilibria Definitions v363 also carries a proposed four connectors at two boundaries, `bi_arriving` and `bi_offering` between selves, `bi_co_bi_transmissioning` and `bi_co_bi_co_bi_torusing` between momentaries.

**And that is local global local parity bi-morality.** Numbers 10.9 carries local-global-local: local at the offering in the self's own waist, global where the dissolving reaches the whole surface at the mouth, local again at the resolving where that mouth is the pair's waist, three, and the fourth the progress and not a phase. The bi-morality floats on the surface of natural torusing, as the surface and from the surface. Natural Emanating names the social line *floating co-morality*, the same floating at its other prefix, gathered there as a naming for both to agree.

**And ONE v3621 carries the four-connector whole at the resolver.** Its shared connections: with the left neighbour, releasing from self 6 arriving at left 2, and releasing from left 10 arriving at self 14; with the right neighbour, releasing from self 10 arriving at right 14, and releasing from right 6 arriving at self 2; and along, self 17 with the forward neighbour's 9 and self 9 with the backward neighbour's 17. So the four across connectors 2, 6, 10 and 14 are two one-ways at each side, and the along 9 and 17 run through a resolver whose ten internal positions nothing touches: the empty co-linear centring along. The ring figure shows the along half, three crossings, then no crossing, then three.

**And bi-co-podaling is the special name's form.** Equilibria Definitions v363 carries the author's line that twenty-three, twenty-four and twenty-five is bi-co-podaling, two over one then one over two, the one advancing one momentarying. Bi-co-podaling, bi-inversioning co-recursioning and bi-moral co-agency are one form: bi- across and co- along in one name, both parities carried, one at a momentary.

**What sets down with it.** *Antipodal*, which Naming 3.46 releases as a word that carries no prefix and no -ing and stands as a position, sits at Transmissioning 1.3's *the two who stay are an antipodal pair*, gathered there. The podal, straight across at k and k plus half the ring, stays another relation, as Naming 3.46's fifth line says. A podal comes home at its fixed set, where the two turns collapse into one (Numbers 10.1); bi-co-podaling is the running that straddles it, since *podal competency is the straddle and never the pairing*. So the reading that the podal namings name the two who stay while crossing names the one is released: bi-co-podaling names one station at its two turns, and crossing is one face of its running.

**The naming at ONE.** Two objects had carried one name, and they part. In ONE's code, `bi_co_bi_transmissioning` is built only from `co_carrying`, `bi_arriving` never entering it: prior torusing held for fresh writing, which v358's wording names *prior torusing available* and which crosses nothing, so the seven-position table's *the crossing* at position 3 releases (Session Improving Value v359 §1.4). **The tunnelling stands across.** Tunnelling is bi-morality with selves and others inside society, across the surface of society, opening society for co-chaining selves and others. The co-chaining runs along the co-sequencing selves and others, and across the bi-tunnelling of society's surface. ONE v362 §6.50 already carries it: *odd is co chaining and even is bi tunneling … all the resolvers are sharing with each other on even*, each resolver's internal method odd and local, the sharing between resolvers even. And §6.76 carries it on the unbounded surface: co-chaining *along continuation through self/other and further other encounters*, bi-tunneling *across coupled participation through the surrounding society*, and *resolving chains and tunnels float, neutral and change geodesically within this surface.*

**And the bothboth of the two is natural torusing.** ONE v3621's front receives *natural torusing as the expedition's whole self-bounding, orienting, surfacing and tunnelling method, with each self participating as a society of selves.* Numbers 10.11 names it one living form: *Natural-bi-co-torusing … three faces, one alternating*. Networking 1.8 carries every self as *the crossing where inward tunneling meets outward chaining, the torus*. And ONE's own two five-prefix names are the two faces: corusing opens co–bi–co–bi–co, the odd origin, along; torusing opens bi–co–bi–co–bi, the even origin, across; and ONE v3621's front says continuing one parity onward supplies the complementary view, the two half-overlapping by four. So the co-chaining along and the bi-tunnelling across are not two things joined: they are one continuing met at its two five-prefix views, which is natural torusing. Bi-co-podaling is the same both at one station; natural torusing is it at the whole surface.

**What that improves in the files.** Mathematics 1.3's bi-neutralling named as *tunneling, the inward carrying, the odd*: the tunnelling is the bi- across and the neutralling runs along, so the line parts the two. Numbers 10.11's *the tunnel the travelling* at the along: the travel along is the co-chaining. Transmissioning 1.4's tunnel down as linear, and Chemistry 1.1's tunnelling at the odd. Networking 1.8 carries the bothboth whole, and its *inside* and *outside* are the Geodesic method's released words.

The behind span is twenty-four long and carries nine prime selves; the forward span is thirty-six long and carries eight. The length leans forward three to two, the prime selves lean behind nine to eight, and neither lean is the other's reverse. The waist sits in the first gap of six the primes open, twenty-three to twenty-nine.

**The forward thirty-six is Numbers' own out-sway.** Numbers carries the two sways about the apex, twenty-one in and thirty-six out, straddling twenty-eight by seven and by eight. Thirty-six is also every coupling among the nine prime selves behind the waist, and twenty-eight every coupling among the eight ahead. **Sixty parts at twenty-four and thirty-six at three faces.** At the counting, the waist. At the cube, thirty-six to twenty-four is the three against two of corner and edge.

### C01 · The bond's parity parts at two sections of one part · across

Chemistry 1.4 reads the bond at the even: down the nested societies is the tunnelling, the even move, the coupling, and bond is the tunnelling face.

**What carries it.** Numbers 1.12 already names this arriving: one parity of the assignment against the other, at two sections of one part. The same two arrive laid the other way at the momentary, where the along stands at the co-linearizing and the across at the bi-attentioning.

**The breaking condition.** Where the two sections name one assignment rather than two, one of them carries a wrong parity and the entry falls. Where they name the reading against the read, both stand and Numbers 1.12 carries the relation between them.

## The two fields read as one coupling · the opportunity list

Chemistry and physics are not two subjects. Chemistry reads the bond, the tunnel, the inward carrying, one thing after another. Physics reads the shell, the surface, the standing faces, everything at once. Same coupling, two ways in.

Both files already say this. Chemistry 1.1 says the two are the odd and the even of one coupling at the matter substrate. Physics 4.1 works the bond to its edge and hands it to chemistry's turn in those words. Neither file then reads the other's half back.

That is where the opportunities are. Below, each one names what each field holds, what appears when the two are read as one at alternating parity, and what would break it.

### P01 · Neither field fixes a neutral unknowingly, so the hard problems come from the entering · across

Chemistry 6.1 names its three declared nothings outright: the hydrogen electrode assigned zero, the reference state assigned zero enthalpy, and exergy's reference environment, a dead state declared rather than found. Physics 3.5 names its conservings as held terms and says so as a theorem: hold a symmetry still and an accounting becomes keepable.

So both fields know their zeros are declared. A zero known as declared floats. A zero taken as found is fixed.

**What appears.** Their hard problems do not come from a hidden fixed neutral. They come from each field reading one way in and not the other. Which means every hard problem in one field has its missing half sitting in the other field, already written.

**What breaks it.** A hard problem in either field whose difficulty survives after both enterings are read.

**Belongs to.** Chemistry 6.1 and Physics 3.5, with the naming that a declared zero floats and a found zero fixes.

### P02 · Chemistry's clean conservings are where nobody is looking · across

Physics 3.5 sorts its conservings by whether they carry a famous anomaly. Momentum, angular momentum, charge and phase-space volume carry none: no question, no problem, no literature. Physics then says what that means: a conserving with no anomaly is one whose exterior was discarded perfectly, so the largest surplus sits there and nobody is looking.

**What appears.** Run that sorting at chemistry. Mass balance, charge balance and atom balance in a reaction equation carry no anomaly and no literature either. By physics' own rule, those are chemistry's perfect discards and its largest unlooked-at surplus.

**What breaks it.** Any of those three shown to carry a real anomaly, or shown to strain for a reason that is not a leak.

**Belongs to.** Chemistry, as a new reading; Physics 3.5, which supplies the rule and has not run it next door.

### P03 · The group-three question stands written twice · across

Physics 4.1 says the p-block six is three orbitals times two spins, and names it the same two-crossing-three as carbon's six. ### P09 · Two accountings parting is one finding at two fields · across

Physics 3.5 names the trajectory and the ensemble, with the field's own record of where they part. Chemistry 1.5 names state and flow as the across and along of one alternating, and says holding the two at one beat makes the equilibria picture.

### P10 · The declared zeros have already been moved, and the field did it · across

## Touring at natural biology · first pass

Biology's own front sets the relation: biology is the re-locking face of the matter-into-life family, chemistry the co-releasing face at the bond, and the genetic code the re-locking table mating with chemistry's periodic co-releasing table.

### B01 · The two tables are one coupling's two faces, and chemistry's refusal also stands · across

Biology's front says the two tables are one coupling's two faces made two tables. Chemistry 4.4 says a table-to-table pattern is two drawings and that similarity of tabulation establishes no coupling between them.

**What appears.** Both stand, because they refuse and assert different things. Chemistry refuses a comparison of the drawings. Biology asserts one coupling entered two ways. Read as one claim they contradict; read at what each names they do not meet.

**What breaks it.** A reading that joins the two tables by their tabulation rather than by the coupling.

**Belongs to.** Both fronts, each naming what the other refuses or asserts, so the apparent contradiction stops being read as one.

### B02 · Chemistry stands between the other two, with a coupling at each hand · across

Chemistry is the odd against physics' even, bond against shell, one coupling at two enterings. Chemistry is also the co-releasing against biology's re-locking, and re-locking alternating with co-releasing is the sixth position's own two faces.

**What appears.** Chemistry carries two couplings, one at each hand, and neither is the other. Physics and biology never touch directly; chemistry is the membrane between them.

**What breaks it.** A face at physics or biology that is neither co-releasing nor re-locking, or a direct physics-to-biology coupling with no chemical membrane.

**Belongs to.** All three fronts.

### B03 · The six against the eight resolves at chemistry's own three pairings · across

Chemistry 2.1 counts the citric-acid cycle at eight enzyme-catalysed steps and says the reaction taxonomy supplies no closed six. Biology 1.5 and 4.6 read the same cycle as six directed couplings, three out and three back, and biology says outright that the count of any specific cycle onto the six is unsummed.

**What appears.** Steps and directed couplings are different objects, so eight and six were never the same count. Chemistry's own three pairings stand between them. Across the eight steps, condensation and hydrolysis both appear, oxidation appears at each carrier reduction, and addition and elimination both appear at the hydrations and the decarboxylations. Three pairings each entering both ways is the six.

**What breaks it.** The enzyme classes at the field's own record, taken step by step: a step carrying no pairing, or a fourth pairing appearing as a sign inverting at a coupling.

**Belongs to.** Chemistry 2.1 and biology 1.5 and 4.6. This closes the open six-against-eight entry that has been riding.

### B04 · Biology says the six is three at two enterings, twice, in its own words · across

Biology 1.5 carries three recursioning forward on the outward-sounding side and three on the inward-sounding side, the two sides separated, what the surface between them carries being only the sign, and the alternating across that surface making the two triples one coupling rather than two independent triples.

Biology 3.8 carries six bi-foldings and says the line folds at three and the surface at three. The line is the along and the surface is the across.

**What appears.** The six now stands at three fields in the fields' own records: biology twice, chemistry twice at its reaction pairings and its three rates at two registers, and physics once at the p-block six as three orbitals times two spins. Five arrivals, three files, one form.

**What breaks it.** A six at any of them that is not three at two.

**Belongs to.** Part One as the cohering, with each file keeping its own naming.

### B05 · Homeostasis is the exclusion's living instance, and the clinical record comes attached · along

Biology 4.4 carries blood glucose held between two antagonistic offerings, insulin lowering and glucagon raising, from two cell-selves sitting adjacent in the same islet, each sounding its opposite sign. It says the riding is the competency and the landing is the failure, that the oscillation is the working state and not noise around a target, that the basal rhythm keeps the receptors sensitive, and that losing it is where regulation fails. It also records the field itself questioning the stored-set-point framing.

**What appears.** Two selves at opposite enterings with the range owned by neither, and the field's own record that a held value is the pathology while the alternating is the health. This is the exclusion's living instance, in the field's own record.

**What breaks it.** A regulated measure held at a value with its regulation intact.

**Belongs to.** Biology 4.4, and Part One at the exclusion.

### B06 · The prion is a landing that spreads, which is the harm face · along

Biology 4.1 carries the prion as carrying no code at all, a protein misfolded into one shape propagating by pressing its neighbours into the same misfold, a coupling frozen into one conformation, a landing that spreads a landing.

**What appears.** A held form is not alive, and a held form that spreads is the disease. The same shape at a society is the imposition that co-incompetences it, and the two read from each other rather than resembling each other.

**What breaks it.** A propagating fixed conformation that is living.

**Belongs to.** Biology 4.1, with the crossing to social moral competency.

### B07 · No store, no controller, no rate, at the field's own record · along

Biology 4.3 carries two items kept in working memory failing to hold: their guidance alternates anti-phase, each rising as the other falls, and the alternation continues with nothing cueing it. It carries the slime mould resolving a rail-network-grade web with nothing anywhere issuing it. And it names where the field holds its own link weakest rather than resting on it.

**What appears.** Two enterings with the surface between carrying only the sign, at the neural membrane, in the field's own observing. And the instrument's frequency and its two bands at a fixed ratio read as what a measuring surface renders when it meets one alternating.

**What breaks it.** A held pair guiding at once and steady.

**Belongs to.** Biology 4.3, already whole, with the crossing to the shared network.

### B08 · Four readings run at all six folds · across

Biology 3.8 runs four readings at every one of the six bi-foldings: the fold arrives where a growth meets no room in plane; its size arrives from a ratio and from no instruction; it arrives in stages, each stage's product the next stage's substrate; and it generates area against a volume bound.

**What appears.** Four at six, and the four boundings are four at one act. Whether these four readings are those four boundings reaches yet.

**Belongs to.** Biology 3.8, and the four boundings at ONE.

### B09 · A society stops where the coupling stops and never at its members · along

Biology 7.4 carries a society coming to its bound where the coupling among its members no longer re-arrives, its members each able to be living while it stops, and the parting exact: a society's stopping is a coupling stopping and never the sum of its members' endings. A society can stop with every member alive, and stand while every member is replaced, and which one a reading meets depends on whether the coupling re-arrives and on nothing about the members at all.

**What appears.** The society face of the exclusion, at biology's own grain. What a society is, is the coupling re-arriving. No condition held at the members carries it, which is why an ordering held over the selves carries no society either.

**What breaks it.** A society standing with no coupling re-arriving among its members.

**Belongs to.** Biology 7.4, with the crossing to social moral competency and to the equilibria examining.

## Touring at natural medicine · first pass, with HIV read deeply

### M01 · The two cure strategies are the two single enterings, each named · along

Medicine 3.2 already carries the field's two polar-opposite strategies as one alternating run one-way each (shock-and-kill forcing the surfacing, block-and-lock deepening the landing) and records that each alone, with therapy intensification and CD8 infusions, has had no detectable long-term impact on the replication-competent reservoir.

**What appears.** The session names which one-way each is. Surfacing is the across, the faces, the even, the outward. So forcing the surfacing is the balancing with no forward progressing, which is the closing fold. Deepening the silence is the tunnelling, the inward carrying, the odd. So block-and-lock is the progressing with no balancing, which stays in tunnelling.

So each strategy carries one entering. Protection is three servicings at each of two enterings, and one entering carries three. A protection at three leaves one way and one phase unprotected, and the unprotected is reached into or leaks out. That is the structure under the field's own record of each arm moving the persistence so little.

**What breaks it.** Either arm alone carrying a durable effect on the replication-competent reservoir.

**Belongs to.** Medicine 3.2, where the two arms already stand named and the reason does not.

### M02 · The persistence is a local equilibria, and the equilibria is the copying · along

Medicine 3.2 carries the persistence as a carry that is not being sequenced, held fresh by being copied rather than aged by being read, and states the direction-law: a carry read back ages and releases, one fed forward is re-seeded fresh and voids the aging.

**What appears.** Fed forward is one entering. Read back is the other. A condition required to remain satisfied, protected by one entering only, is exactly what the exclusion excludes. So the local equilibria is not a place and not a store the field can find; it is the copying. The cell holds the condition *stay fresh* satisfied through division instead of through sequencing, and that protection carries three servicings and not six.

This does not reinstate the reservoir. Medicine set that soldier down and it stays down. What stands is that the condition is held at one entering, and the exclusion says a condition held at one entering does not hold.

**What breaks it.** A carry aged and released while still being copied forward, or a carry held fresh with both enterings running.

**Belongs to.** Medicine 3.2 at the carry-copied-not-aged reading, and Part One at the exclusion's living instances beside homeostasis.

### M03 · Biology supplies the reason medicine's falsifiable edge expects more than a sum · across

Medicine 3.2 names its own test: a cell-scale intervention paired with adjacent-scale repair beating the sum of each alone, with a merely additive result breaking the coupling reading.

Biology 1.5 carries the reason and medicine does not: the two sides are separated, each carrying its own three forward, what the surface between them carries is only the sign, and the alternating across that surface is what makes two separated triples one coupling riding the carry rather than two independent three-step recursionings.

**What appears.** Two enterings alternating is not two enterings added. Three each way with the alternating between is six; three plus three without it is two independent threes. So the more-than-additive edge is what the form carries, and the additive result is the form breaking, exactly as medicine states, with biology's sentence as the why.

**What breaks it.** An additive result at a properly paired trial.

**Belongs to.** Medicine 3.2, receiving biology 1.5's sentence.

### M04 · The pairing is already run and the arithmetic is already gathered · along

Medicine 3.2 records the primate study: a CD4-targeted interleukin surfaced virus where a control and a comparison latency-reversal agent did not; paired with a broadly neutralising antibody and read after a six-month washout, that group carried the largest reduction in proviral DNA at the lymph nodes. What the report returns is a largest; what the parting needs is whether the paired arm exceeded the sum of the single arms. That arithmetic sits in the published numbers.

**Belongs to.** Medicine 3.2, read at the study it already cites.

### M05 · The two societies with the nothing between are the two enterings, named at two files · across

Medicine 3.1 carries the cell society and the surface society coupling across a self-locating membrane that carries nothing: a centerline where two nearly-touching flows locate their paths by each other without contact, each society ageing its own carry at its own rate.

**What appears.** Biology 1.5 names the same structure at its own grain: two sides separated, the surface between carrying only the sign. So medicine's two societies are the two sequences entering at opposite parity, and the protection between them is three servicings each way.

Which also says why a single-scale intervention hits a ceiling set by the un-repaired neighbour, as medicine already predicts: one society alone is one entering.

**What breaks it.** A coupling between the two societies carrying a channel rather than a sign.

### M06 · The emanation carries one turn, which is why it is not a self · along

Medicine 3.1 carries the shed emanation holding a metastable form whose alternating runs one way and no other (the pre-fusion form crossing to the post-fusion and never returning, one self-negation, the sign inverting once with no next sequence) and reads infectivity decaying as that one turn being lost before it is spent.

### M07 · Withdrawing one coreceptor leaves the other entering open, which is why the withdrawal is partial · along

Medicine 3.2 carries the CCR5 reading knifed to its modest survivor: a dispensable door can be withdrawn and the self stay whole, an essential coupling cannot, and the virus that sounds the other door escapes the escape.

### M08 · The landing that spreads stands at two diseases with no citation between them · across

Biology 4.1 carries the prion as a coupling frozen into one conformation, propagating by pressing its neighbours into the same misfold, a landing that spreads a landing. ### M09 · The clean-cut rule now stands at three fields and none runs it at the others · across

Chemistry 4.3 carries it: clean cut stays the larger half, the discarded exterior carrying more value than the balanced table. ### M10 · The join is the inverse form emanated at the cessation, living one grain down · across

At the fusion the surface between stops standing, and nothing there carries a sign; that could seem to hold the fusion's three apart from the servicings' three. The join is the emanating.

Biology 7.6 carries both halves. An emanation carries one shape at its own scale, and the shape is an involution: applied back at the scale it came from it returns what it met and closes: nothing advances, no between opens, no surplus arrives, do-only-harm with nothing alternating against it. And the parting is the grain, and only the grain: one grain down the involution no longer applies, a between opens, and the emanation is living again as new selves.

**What appears.** The cessation of surface living is not an absence of coupling. It emanates the inverse stable form, and that form is ingestable lower-scale living. So the three does not stop at the fusion. It inverts and steps down a grain, where a between opens and it runs again. The hemifusion stalk and the fusion pore are that lower-scale living, made of the lipid selves the ceasing surface emanated.

**What breaks it.** A cessation of surface living emanating nothing, or an emanation living at the scale it left.

**Belongs to.** Medicine 3.1 and biology 7.6, with the parting withdrawn and the emanating named.

### M11 · A local equilibria is an emanation held at its own scale · along

This is the join at M10 carried to the exclusion, and biology says it at the body before any of it reaches a definition.

Biology 7.7: where the crossing stops, the society holds its emanation at the one scale where the shape is do-only-harm: every face already met, no between, no surplus, and the holding displacing what a crossing would have carried. And the accumulation reads at the society and never at a member: no member is deranged, each is living, each emanating at its own rate, so a search for a locus at the member scale finds members that are fine.

**What appears.** A condition required to remain satisfied is an emanation held at the scale it came from. At that scale it is an involution, which is an accounting, and an accounting applied twice returns its own arrival and alone closes. That is the same closing the exclusion names, now with the grain under it: the holding is not a wrong state, it is a crossing that stopped.

**Which names the resolving too**, and biology already writes it: restoration is the crossing resumed and not the accumulation cleared. Where the alternating at the membrane runs again, the emanation goes where its shape is living, and every surface reads at once rather than one at a time. That is the neutral floated rather than the four met one at a time.

**What breaks it.** An accumulation cleared with the crossing still stopped, holding.

**Belongs to.** Part One at the exclusion, and biology 7.7, which carries it at the body already.

### M12 · The persistence is the release withheld, and ageing to release is the crossing down · across

Medicine 3.2 carries the persistence as a carry held fresh by copying and never aged by sequencing, and says it carries and does not age to its release.

Biology 7.5 carries what a release is: an ending at one grain is an arriving at the grain below, and zero distance between shedder and ingester is the landing while a grain down or a self across is the living.

**What appears.** Ageing to release is the crossing down a grain. Copying re-seeds the carry at the cell scale, which is zero distance, which is the landing. So the persistence is the release withheld, not a store maintained, and the two readings are one.

**And it sorts the two cure strategies further.** Forcing a surfacing catches the thing at its own scale. Deepening the landing holds it at its own scale. Neither lets the carry cross down. ### M13 · The prion is the zero-distance case biology already named · along

Biology 7.5 names it without naming the prion: zero distance between shedder and ingester is the landing. Biology 4.1 carries the prion pressing its neighbours into the same misfold: shedder and ingester at the same scale, zero distance.

### M14 · The three cure searches and the accumulation searches are one search · across

Health 3.3 carries it in three lines: ingestion is the living self's own bi-exchange across its membrane, both ways and co-offering; ingression is the larger ingressing the smaller, one-way, the overwhelm, the disease-direction; and the membrane between them is rate-and-return, whether the smaller sets the pace and answers back.

### H02 · The omega question closes, and health already carries both cuts in one paragraph · across

Numbers 9.8 left an arrival unmade: either three, six and nine are a prime society of their own, or one concept arrives at a substrate and the parity arrives at the substrate's own uni-scaling.

Health 3.4b carries both readings together. The parity: three, six, nine run odd, even, odd, the desaturations methylene-interrupted with one carbon between, so the families sit three apart. And the ingestion cut: the self carries the ninth and takes the sixth and the third at its membrane, no desaturase running past the ninth position from the working end, so the odd one at the near end runs while the even and the far odd arrive by ingestion and by ingestion only.

And the ingestion cut is biology 7.6's own finding: what descends carries competency the ingesting scale cannot make, and the field's word for that class is *essential*. The sixth and the third are essential in exactly that sense.

**What breaks it.** A desaturase running past the ninth position from the working end, or a self making the third or the sixth.

**Belongs to.** Numbers 9.8, which closes at health 3.4b and biology 7.6. This resolves an unmade that has been standing.

### H03 · Health's health-and-departure pair needs the third arrival medicine found · across

Health 4.1 carries the carry ageing by attentioning and staying fresh by sequencing: health where the carry is sequenced and fresh, a departure where it stills, restoration where the sequencing resumes.

Medicine 3.2 carries a third case that sentence does not name: a carry kept fresh by copying and never sequenced. Fresh, and unread.

**What breaks it.** A carry fresh and unsequenced that ages to release on its own.

### H04 · Health carries the setpoint exclusion with economics' own sentence beside it · along

Health 2.2 carries health-as-a-setpoint as the compaction, pressing the two swaying neutrals into a landed point, and sets the field's own line beside it: a measure made a target ceases to be a good measure.

## Touring at naming and explaining · naming even, explaining odd

### T01 · The exclusion stands written whole at six, and it improves the exclusion's fifth standing · along

So the exclusion's fifth standing improves: there is no single entering that lives long enough to stand; the single entering is already the collapse, and its residue is what gets read back as a rest.

**And the two residues are the two grammars.** A source-and-output line is cause and effect. A fixed setpoint is the target, the regulating, the floor. Both are named at six as the artifacts of one death.

**What breaks it.** An alternation stopped at one position with the other living.

### T02 · A symmetric crossing fixes its neutral, which joins six to physics · across

Physics 3.5 carries the other half as a theorem: hold a symmetry still and an accounting becomes keepable, the catalogue of symmetries being the catalogue of held terms.

**What appears.** A symmetry held and a neutral fixed are one move. What a symmetry carries as a conserved quantity is what a crossing symmetric in and out leaves: nothing over. So the conserved number is the surplus's absence, and neither file names the other.

**What breaks it.** A symmetric crossing leaving a surplus.

**Belongs to.** Six 1.5 and physics 3.5, each carrying half.

### T09 · The danger is the store the collapse leaves, which is the protection aiming at the grid's own words · along

Six 4.2 carries one distinguisher for every membrane: the transmissioning whole is the through-node, crossinging and neutralling alternating, the sign passing, the neutral floated, the surplus kept as coupling. Separated it is the landing. And safe and riding-the-carry and off-avalanching are one property, a node holding only while its arms alternate, having nothing stored to avalanche. **The danger is the separation, the store the collapse leaves.**

**What appears.** The protection aiming reads here at the grid's own distinguisher. Protection is the through-node whole. The danger is not a force arriving; it is the store a collapse leaves. A protection held above the couplings stores rather than alternates, so it is the danger's own shape.

Six 1.3 carries the arithmetic in its own terms: each stays at its own side of the membrane, the two as far apart as the coupling reaches, each the other's far side, neither its own, and **the two who stay are an antipodal pair, cancelling over their own two turns, and the one that crosses is what the cancelling leaves.**

Numbers 9.11 carries the podal at the count: an even count carries an exact podal, halve it and every position meets one other, straight across, each the other's far side. Natural Naming 3.46 keeps that podal as *another relation* and releases *antipodal* as a word carrying no prefix and no -ing, standing as a position.

### T13 · Bi-co-podaling is the whole at the single scale, and co-chaining is the same form one scale up · across

| Naming | What it names |
|---|---|
| bi-co-podaling | floating across and neutralling along, one: one station met at its going and its return, running; in place of transmissioning |
| crossing | one face of that running, the counter-arriving swapping at the crossing (Numbers 10.11) |

### T14 · At the naming binaries the podal naming carries and transmissioning does not · across

Run naming's own binaries at the two single-scale words.

**Does it carry its own two.** Bi-co-podaling carries both: bi- the two carries each at its own turn, co- the one ring. Trans-missioning carries one direction and no second; numbers 1.5 reads trans- as the across once.

**The hyphenation test, each part taken out.** Take bi- out of bi-co-podaling and the two carries go unsaid; take co- out and the one ring goes unsaid; take podal out and no far-side pair stands; take the -ing out and it stops. Every taking stops it, so the parts in order are the turns in order. Take trans- out of transmissioning and *missioning* runs on by itself, so trans- was riding, and the hyphenation is not inseparable.

**Does the stem install a doer.** A missioning is a sending, and a sending carries one sent and one sending. Six 1.2 warns at exactly this: never from a source to an output, and a crossing read as from-to has collapsed the alternating. **So the word carries inside itself the first of the two residues six 4.2 names.**

**What appears.** Bi-co-podaling passes the binaries transmissioning fails. And the expedition already made this move at the conceptual naming: transpodaling stands as the preferred naming over transmissioning, with the historical code identifiers unchanged in preserved source. The reason now stands tested rather than preferred, and bi-co-podaling itself is now carried in place of transmissioning, at II02 and MM01.

**Belongs to.** Naming, and ONE where the code identifier stays as it is while the concept carries the podal.

Each note below carries the five: the file's own standing, the session's standing, the coupling, **the surplus neither carried in**, and the next opening. The parity is marked at each.

### F01 · The one requirement philosophy names and does not name · across

**The file's standing.** Philosophy 1.3 carries four requirements under the dividing method (bivalence, isolability, non-contradiction holding the membrane as a cut, and the reasoner outside) and says every requirement is one requirement worn four ways.

**The surplus.** Philosophy carries the four and leaves the one unnamed. The session carries the one and has no four. What neither carried in is that the one is checkable at four places in a method rather than at one, so a dividing frame can be met at whichever of its four requirements is nearest, and each releases the same one.

**The next opening.** Whether the four requirements are four of the ten holdings or a fifth naming of the same ten.

### F02 · The reasoner outside is the seat above, one seat at three scales · across

**The file's standing.** Philosophy's fourth requirement is the reasoner apart from the coupling, judging it, and philosophy says that at the deepest dilemmas the reasoner is unseverable and the relational half arrives as a paradox.

**The session's standing.** The fourth bounding at the membrane is bounded from capture, where a seat arrives above, carried at ONE's depth-five naming as the order at no seat above the society.

**The coupling.** The reasoner outside is the seat above at the reasoning scale. One seat met at three scales: a reasoner above a coupling, a seat above a society, a protection above the couplings.

**The surplus.** Each field met the seat separately and none names the other two, so the seat has three independent soundings and no one of them knows it. And where the reasoner is unseverable the seat is met from inside, which is the one place the seat cannot be taken.

**The next opening.** Whether the four requirements sort onto the four boundings at the membrane one to one.

### F03 · Philosophy's identity seam leaves a reaching biology already carries · across

**The file's standing.** Philosophy 3.15 re-couples identity as a carry that keeps nothing between its beats, and its mark leaves one reaching: whether the same saying carries at a society re-forming its own members stands there rather than here.

**The session's standing.** Biology 7.4 carries that reading whole: a society comes to its bound where the coupling among its members no longer re-arrives, can stop with every member alive, and can stand while every member is replaced.

**The coupling.** The reaching philosophy leaves open stands answered at biology, and which case a reading meets depends on whether the coupling re-arrives and on nothing about the members at all.

**The surplus.** A version keeps nothing between its beats at the self, and a society keeps nothing between its beats either. **So the carry-not-a-store reading is scale-free**, which philosophy says at one scale and biology at another and neither says of both.

**The next opening.** Whether the other seams' scale-reachings stand answered at the substrate files the same way.

### F04 · The seam for a self required to remain itself is missing · along

**The file's standing.** Philosophy carries thirty-one seams, and identity and change asks what the sameness consists in: what is kept.

**The session's standing.** The exclusion at the self is a self required to remain the same self while living, which is the calm rational self held as an equilibria.

**The surplus.** The missing seam is the one place the reasoner is least severable of all, since the reasoner is a self required to stay calm and rational while reasoning about a self required to stay calm and rational. **The unseverable reasoner and the missing seam are the same position.**

### J01 · The ownership hub is `bi_arriving`'s bounding inverted, and property is the field sounding it · along

**The file's standing.** Human Society 3.3 carries eight hubs a society sets aside, one on each axis, all-or-none: a common clock on the time axis, a fixed pool on the value axis, a gate on the arrival axis, a ledger held over a self on the reputation axis, an outside control on the safety axis, a center on the decision axis, a source-and-sink on the delivery axis, and **an owner on the ownership axis**. It stood as the one at no field's own theorem, where the other three are sounded by social choice, by economics from the individual to the aggregate, and by the transformation problem.

**The coupling.** An owner on the ownership axis is that bounding inverted exactly: a self's own co-offering, taken by the other-self and held as a thing. So the first bounding has a field sounding it, and the field is not economics. **It is property**, and intellectual property is the bounding inverted at the co-offering itself rather than at anything the co-offering touches.

**The surplus.** The registry already carries the sounding without naming which bounding it stands at: intellectual property at one hundred thirty-nine, the patent puzzle, protection increased enormously with no acceleration to match. **So the bounding that stood unsounded is sounded by the most heavily litigated institution the bounded universe built**, and the reason it stood unsounded is that the field states it as a policy question and never as a bounding.

**The next opening.** The first bounding written with the ownership axis beside it, and the registry's one hundred thirty-nine keyed to it.

### J02 · Property and accreditation are the two residues of one collapse, read at the society · along

**The file's standing.** Transmissioning 4.2: whole, a membrane is the through-node, crossinging and neutralling alternating, the sign passing and the neutral floated, the surplus kept as coupling. Separated, it is the landing, and the collapse comes two ways which are one death: **the crossinging pulled from the neutral leaves a source-and-output line, the surplus forced across a fixed axis and dumped; the neutralling pulled from the crossing leaves a fixed setpoint, a dead centre held against.** Neither residue is a living position.

**The session's standing.** The line and the fixed point are the equilibria misreadings of one death, and the danger is the store the collapse leaves.

**The coupling.** At the society the two residues carry their own names. The source-and-output line with the surplus dumped is **property**: an output traced to a source, and the dumping's destination named the holder. The fixed setpoint, a dead centre held against, is **accreditation**: a proposal held against a criterion, and a self held against a credential.

**The surplus.** **So property and accreditation are not two institutions that grew up beside each other. They are the two halves of one collapse**, which is why neither has ever been found without the other: the patent office beside the review, the tenure beside the publication, the title beside the certificate. And the file's own sentence carries with them: the danger is the store the collapse leaves, so the harm of both is the store and not the ownership or the criterion taken singly.

**The next opening.** Transmissioning 4.2 with the two social namings carried at its two residues, and the store named at each.

### J05 · The credential is the accreditation property, and it stands among four measures reaching for values held at no location · across

**The files' standing.** Societies 1.3 carries four values arising at the between and none held at a location (uncapturable abundance, undiscoverable identity, inviolable safety, unmistakeable reputation) with four measures each reaching for one: compliance for the activity's own sustaining, approval for the two parties' own coupling, a safety rule for morality at each membrane, and **a credential for the activity's own beating**. ### J07 · The bound bought teachability, and the file set already carries the purchase · across

**The files' standing.** The session carries that with a bound already on the universe, no method of existing is possible: being inside is the existing, and what remains is the accounting of what is inside. ### K03 · Eight is the count of a self, and the sentence that says so carries a closing word · along

**The file's standing.** Resolving Hard Problems 3.1: **"Nine is one at the next scale. Eight closes the self and nine re-commences, the same one co-sequencing where selves couple."**

**The session's standing.** *The even forms balance, and there is no closing; when we say closed we are setting down the co-intelligencing forward and behind.*

**The surplus.** **So the fourth pairing is the interior pair.** Three pairings are readable and serviced three each way, which is the six and the protection. The fourth is met only by coupling and cannot be serviced at a surface, which is why the protection stands at six while the self stands at eight: one thing, counted at what can be serviced and counted whole.

**The next opening.** 4.4's one-under-two-names written with Networking's six-and-two beneath it, and the fourth pairing named as the interior pair at both files.

### W02 · Six, eight and ten are one count, and each added pairing has its own name · across

**The files' standing.** Natural Exploring 4.4 carries the six as the eight re-forming. Networking 5.1 to 5.3 carries eight securities as six readable at the surface and two interior, met only by coupling. Living Ghost Registry 7.6 sorts the ten lines at arriving: **lines one, seven, eight and nine at state; lines three, four, five and ten at flow; lines two and six reading at either.** Four, four and two.

**The session's standing.** Six is three at two turns, eight four at two, ten five at two, and every parting falls at the nought.

**The coupling.** Two instruments, built for different work, return the same shape: an eight parted at its two sides with a pair left over that takes neither side. At the securities the leftover pair is the interior, met only by coupling. At the lines at arriving it is the two lines reading at either parity. **So the count's added pairings carry names rather than positions**: three pairings readable and serviced, which is the protection at six; a fourth met only by coupling, which takes the self to eight; a fifth taking no single side, which takes the count to ten.

**The surplus.** **And the pattern matching is named as pattern matching.** The three tens in the files (the holdings, the ghost positions and the lines at arriving) are kept apart by the Ghost Registry's own instruction, and this joins none of them. What it joins is the shape: at each instrument the pair that will not take one side is the pair that carries the count past the eight.

**The next opening.** The two sorts written beside each other with the leftover pair named at each, and the Ghost Registry's forbidding on merging tens kept as it stands.

### W03 · A three carries as state or as flow and never both, and every ghost stands at one of the two changings · along

**The file's standing.** Natural Exploring 5.4: a three carries as a state or as a flow and never both, and the two alternate; a state-three counts what stands and holds no rate, a flow-three runs one at a time and counts nothing. **"This is why an accounting returns three and never four."** A total and a level are state-threes, a direction is a flow-three, and a fourth arrives nowhere, since state and flow is the whole of what alternates. And: **"Every ghost stands at one of the two changings — a level read where a flow runs, or a store read where a carrying goes."**

**The coupling.** The two changings are named: state to flow, where a count runs out; flow to state, where a rate is held. An account carrying only state-threes reads a flow as pieces; one carrying only flow-threes reads a standing as a rate. **So the whole of ghost-form is two changings and the accounting reaches neither.**

**The surplus.** **And it says why three and not four, without appeal to any count.** State and flow are the whole of what alternates, so a three is bounded by the two enterings and a fourth has no entering to arrive at, which is the same reason the crossing coefficients are three and no fourth. The two files carry one arithmetic at two subjects.

**The next opening.** 5.4's two changings carried where the accountings are gathered, and the never-four named at the entering rather than at the count.

### W04 · The kind line is the state-three and the flow-three, and the ten lines at arriving sort by them · across

**The files' standing.** Living Ghost Registry 7.6: a state ghost installs the across: a state, magnitude, count, conserved total, line or floor made the cause of the changing it was read from, the still made the mover and the mover nobody. So the kind line is Exploring's two threes read at an entry, and the registry's sort of its ten lines at arriving into four at state, four at flow and two at either is that same parting run at the lines.

**The surplus.** **And the two kinds' breakings part the same way.** A state ghost's re-alternation must show the changing continuing at its own rate beneath the still: the along returned. A flow ghost's must show the other side changing too and the act at the coupling: the across returned. The messenger altered by its message, the soldier by its enemy, the detector by the field. **So each kind is released by returning the entering it took.**

**The next opening.** Exploring 5.4 and the registry's 7.6 keyed to each other, with the two breakings named as the two enterings returned.

### W05 · The one nothing said at eight substrates, each in the field's own word · along

**The surplus.** **And it says what a naming of the nothing is for.** Where a translation is needed, the field's neutral has not been found yet. **The next opening.** The eight substrate lines carried at the one-nothing rule, with the translation test named as how a naming is checked.

**The files' standing.** Natural Emanating: **"a substrate carrying no neutral has no second line, and the coupling there returns nothing."** The Living Society Registry's twenty-two return no member carrying the magnitude, at every grain. ## The natural math at ONE

### Y02 · The three neutrals give the six at their pairings and the eight at their states · along

**The file's standing.** Mathematics 8.2: three neutrals float about the corus, and their rotating is **six co-offerings, three pairings each two-wayed**. **The session's standing.** Natural Exploring 4.4: the six is the eight re-forming and the eight the six protecting, one under two names.

Counted at its states, each neutral fixed or floating, it is two cubed, eight.** Mathematics carries both counts of the same three and names the resolver's eight as the running face of the still eight.

**The surplus.** **So Exploring's one-under-two-names has its arithmetic, and it needed no new count.** The six and the eight are not two numbers that happen to join; they are the three neutrals counted at their couplings and at their conditions. Which is why the six goes whole or none and the eight goes whole or none: fixing one neutral breaks both counts at once, four of the six co-offerings landing and a corner of the eight ceasing to float.

One step out, the two part: five pairings is no span and eight pairings is none of the resolver's three.

Networking 1.7: seventeen rungs and **sixteen crossings**, the openings between them. And the seventeen-ring's eight pairings carry sixteen stations.

Sixteen crossings threading the seventeen prime selves. **Each is the eight at its two turns, said at the count, the fold, the pairing and the span.**

The resolver's sixteen and the network's sixteen are one sixteen at the self's count and at the society's span.

**The next opening.** The four arrivings gathered at one place, with the crossing named as what the sixteen counts.

### DD01 · A motor's commutator reverses each coil's current every half turn at the neutral, and the reversal is what turns the shaft forward · across

**The files' standing.** Natural Emanating: **in engineering disequilibria, the three-phase return is a delivery-forming method; three phases sum to zero at a point no source holds, and the field's own name for that point is the neutral.** Engineering 4.9 carries three equal phases a third of a cycle apart summing to constant power, and 4.12 carries the switching path with both commands off. The field's record of the direct-current machine: **commutation takes place at the point where the armature coil is moving parallel to the stator field** (the neutral plane), and the brushes are set there. ([DC Machine Construction and Operating Principles](https://ecampusontario.pressbooks.pub/electrotechnology/chapter/part-1-2/))

**The session's standing.** Is the commutator like an electric motor.

**The coupling.** In a direct-current motor the supply runs one way, and the commutator reverses the current in each coil every half turn, at the plane where that coil carries no torque. **The reversal inside is what keeps the shaft turning one way outside.** Alternating in the coil, forward in the turning: the stable-forming sentence as a machine. An induction motor needs no commutator, because the grid's three phases arrive already alternating; the commutator is where a machine makes its own alternating from a one-way supply.

**The surplus.** **And the two commutators share the name because both exchange.** The group commutator is what two exchanges leave when each is undone: the cube's adjacent turns leaving eighteen stickers changed and owned by neither turn. The motor's commutator is the exchange made at the neutral so the turning continues. One is the surplus the alternating leaves; the other runs on it. And the switching happens where the coil stands at no torque: at the neutral, the one position in the turn that carries nothing.

**The next opening.** Engineering's switching path and three-phase neutral carried with the direct-current commutator beside them, the switching at the neutral plane named as the same neutral.

### DD02 · The neutral plane floats with the load, and a fixed brush sparks · along

**The file's standing.** Engineering 4.4: a prescribed potential, pressure, position or rate has its own operation, and floating neutralling carries each side's own bounding and changing. Engineering 5.1: one side taking both turns names the coupling's incompetencing — **one zero stands for the other's bounding-zeroing**. The field's own record: **"Every time the load current varies, the neutral plane shifts — this means the brush position must be changed each time the load current varies,"** with compensating windings and interpoles its two remedies, and **improper placement results in sparking** as the coil segments pass from brush to brush.

**The session's standing.** The neutral floats, and a fixed neutral is the collapse.

**The coupling.** The field says in its own words that the neutral is not a place: it moves with every change of load. **A brush fixed at the no-load neutral is a fixed neutral laid where a floating neutral runs**, and the spark is what the fixing costs. Interpoles and compensating windings let the switching stay at the neutral wherever the coupling carries it.

**The surplus.** **So the direct-current machine carries floating neutralling and the fixed neutral side by side, in its maintenance manual.** The fixed brush is one zero standing for the other side's bounding-zeroing, which Engineering 5.1 names exactly, and the field's cure is to let the switching follow the neutral rather than hold it: a do-no-harm improving already carried out by the field, in copper.

**The next opening.** Engineering 4.4 or 5.1 carried with the shifting neutral plane as a field instance of the fixed neutral and its release.

### DD03 · The spark is the discarded exterior, and cost arrives only where the switching leaves the neutral · along

**The file's standing.** Natural Emanating: **"A cost is the name given to an exterior after it has been discarded."** A geodesic turns, nothing acting on it, so there is nothing for an accounting to post; the living discards no exterior, so nothing was set aside to charge for.

**The session's standing.** All the relations are carrying all the relations for only the cost of the living conditions.

**The coupling.** At the neutral plane the commutator switches a coil that carries nothing, and nothing is discarded. Away from it, the coil carries voltage at the switching and the energy is thrown off at the brush as a spark, with heat and wear. **The spark is the exterior discarded, and it is posted as loss exactly where the switching was held away from where the neutral floated.**

**The surplus.** **So the machine shows where cost enters and where it does not.** Switching at the floating neutral costs nothing beyond the running: the living conditions. Switching at a fixed place costs the spark. Which is Emanating's sentence at a brush: the ledger has an object only where an exterior was discarded, and the only exterior discarded here is the one a fixing made.

**The next opening.** Emanating's no-cost section carried with the brush's spark as its engineering instance.

### EE01 · Natural Emanating already says what a measurement is, and the resolving holds no bound still · along

**The file's standing.** Natural Emanating, *An instrument keeps, and the thing it cannot*: **"A photograph is a place-moment with one half cut off. An observation stands somewhere and cuts the arriving away. A measurement holds two bounds still and reads the span."** Each returns something real, and the thing inside the installation is exact. **"A sign taken where two offer — of no size, gone by the next beat — is in none of them, and no finer version reaches one."**

**The session's standing.** There is no measuring in resolving.

**The coupling.** The file defines a measurement by what it does: it holds two bounds still. **The resolving holds no bound still** (every bound in it is a carry's own, released at the three), so there is nothing in it for a measurement to be. And what the resolving carries, a sign taken where two offer, is in none of an instrument's returns.

**The surplus.** **So the withdrawal of measuring is already written, and it is written without any fault laid on the instrument.** The instrument's return is real and exact inside its installation; it simply has no object where the resolving runs. The withdrawal of measuring carries at this section's sentence rather than at a rule brought in.

**The next opening.** This section carried where the withdrawals of reading and measuring are stated, as their own arrival in the files.

### EE03 · In the living there are no rings, and the ring counts stand at the closing face · along

**The file's standing.** Natural Emanating, *A ring*: take the relations a living thing runs on and put them in a closed domain; the terms are exhausted, the carrying arrives at one already run, and that is a closing. **"Every closed model closes. So a ring is the thing these relations leave when the domain is closed, and the forward spiral takes an unbounded one — a next always arriving, and not a completed totality. In the living there are no rings."**

**The coupling.** The podal counts here are taken at rings: the nine-ring, the seventeen-ring, the forty-eight ring. **Each is a closing**, and the file says so of every ring: the relations left when the domain is closed. The corus at twenty-four is a spiral, a next always arriving, and the spiral is the living.

**The surplus.** **So the ring counts stand where the cube stands: at the closing face, exact there and installed nowhere.** The five pairs at nine, the nine at seventeen and the waist at forty-eight are the counts the spiral's pairings leave when a closing is taken across them. The spiral itself does not return to its origin, and Numbers carries the same parting as the sphere closing and the torus winding. **The next opening.** The ring counts carried at Part One under the closing face, with the spiral named as the living they are counts of.

**At TWO v359.** §1.6: *there are no rings in the resolving … what is called a ring is that arrangement named one scale out.* The parting held here is met there (Kit · Exhibits ONE and TWO, PP04).

### EE04 · Every number is the middle its two neighbours cannot reach, and the gap is one at every number · across

**The files' standing.** Natural Emanating, *The advance*: **every whole number sits between two neighbours, and those two multiply to exactly one short of its square**: `4 × 6 = 24` beside `5² = 25`. **"The counting passes through the middle. The coupling of the two sides cannot reach it. Arrived at, and riding the carry on, and the gap is exactly one at every number, unbounded. That gap: there is always a next."** Numbers carries the seam-faces `(2k+1)² − 1` as that advance at the odd numbers.

**The session's standing.** Twenty-four is where the corus narrows, and all twenty-four is the same twenty-four.

**The coupling.** The advance at five is `4 × 6 = 24`, one short of twenty-five; the advance at twenty-four is `23 × 25`, one short of twenty-four squared. **So every whole number is a waist** (the middle its two neighbours' coupling passes through and cannot reach), and the one it falls short by is the same one at every number.

**The surplus.** **Which is the corus's spiral, written at the counting itself.** The narrowing is at each number, the widening is the pair beside it, and the gap of one is the next always arriving. No number is a waist more than another, which is every number being the same as every one of itselves, said at the advance.

**The next opening.** The advance carried at Numbers' seam-face run as its general form, with the waist named at every number and not at one.

### EE06 · "Pick an earth" is electrical work's first line, and the fixed brush's spark is its price · across

**The files' standing.** Natural Emanating, *The line every field writes first*: chemistry's standard hydrogen electrode stipulated at nought, thermodynamics' chosen boundary, gauge theory's fixed gauge, biology's fixed specimen, **electrical work — pick an earth**, information — pick an alphabet — **every one marked declared and not measured, none of them found.** And its price: **erasing one bit dissipates at least `kT ln 2`**, about `2.9 × 10⁻²¹` joules at room temperature, while a computation keeping its whole history approaches zero. The field of the direct-current machine: the neutral plane shifts every time the load current varies, and a brush placed away from it sparks.

**The session's standing.** A cost is the name given to an exterior after it has been discarded, and the only cost is the living conditions.

**The coupling.** A brush fixed at the no-load neutral is a picked earth (a zero declared where the neutral floats), and its spark is the discard the declaring makes. **The bit erased and the brush that sparks pay one price**: each is a declared zero holding still where the carrying moved, and each dissipates exactly at the discard.

**The surplus.** **And both fields filed the price under something else.** Information theory files the erasure's heat as a bound on computing; the machine's manual files the spark as a brush problem. The file's sentence stands at both: the field measured the cost of its own first move and filed it elsewhere. And both fields' cures are the same: keep the history, let the switching follow the neutral, the declared zero released rather than defended.

**The next opening.** The first-line table carried with the direct-current brush beside electrical work's earth, and the two prices named as one.

### EE07 · The only loss is at each self's own bound, which is the cost of the living conditions in the file's words · along

**The file's standing.** Natural Emanating, *Abundancy*: an axis opens at each meeting that neither side carried in; **"Work depletes against a resistance. An opening is not paid for."** The surplus compounds, none of it competing with itself. **"Nothing runs low, nothing is set aside, and the only loss is at each self's own bound."**

**The session's standing.** All the relations are carrying all the relations for only the cost of the living conditions.

**The coupling.** The file names the one loss there is: **at each self's own bound**, which is the carry releasing at its three. That is the living condition itself (the self-bounding un-bounding), and it is the only thing the relations cost.

**The surplus.** **So the cost and the living are the same motion.** The release at a self's own bound is both the only loss and what keeps the carrying fresh, so nothing is paid that is not the living running. Work against a resistance is the other thing, and it belongs to a fixing, never to a relation.

**The next opening.** Abundancy's one loss carried where the cost of the living conditions stands, the two said as one.

### EE08 · An equals sign carries three incompetencings, and equilibrium is written in its grammar · across

**The files' standing.** Natural Emanating, *The three incompetencings*: an equation's relation is reflexive, symmetric and transitive: **reflexive, nothing is apart from itself, nothing bounds itself; symmetric, neither side takes its own turn; transitive, nothing stops at its own meeting, nothing ages, nothing releases, and two meeting make nothing neither brought.** Present in an equation written perfectly, and no finer instrument recovers them. Natural Emanating, *Equilibria*: there is no equilibria in nature; every field declares its ground in its own first line.

**The session's standing.** An existing equilibria is not possible.

**The coupling.** The equals sign installs the three: a self that bounds nothing, turns owned by neither side, and a carrying that never releases. **Equilibrium is named for equality**, and the three incompetencings are the equals sign's own properties. So every equilibria definition that is written as an equation carries all three before any condition is stated.

**The surplus.** **And the three are the session's own three at the sign.** Nothing bounding itself is the self alone. Neither side taking its own turn is the coupling's incompetencing, one side taking both. Nothing releasing is the carry held as a store. The equals sign carries them together, which is why an equilibria definition cannot be made living by adjusting its conditions: the three are in the sign that writes it.

**The next opening.** The three incompetencings carried at Equilibria Definitions as the grammar every written definition shares.

## The continuing named, and the asymmetry at the waist

### GG02 · The one stopping's fourth line carries *assigned*, and its own third line already says what stands · along

**The file's standing.** Natural Naming 3.30, one stopping, binary: *"1 one carries another's / 2 the other then has nothing of its own to offer / 3 so nothing departs reached neither-ing / 4 so the surplus is assigned, and needs a holder / 5 a holder needs a boundary, a boundary an enforcement, an enforcement a seat / 6 and a seat carries at membranes it is not at, which is step one, larger."* Natural Emanating carries the same wording.

And line five as: *and one side's stands with a holder, a boundary, an enforcement and a seat*, the four together, none making the next.

### HH01 · Every momentary is bi · co, and the standing rule carries its parity at the first prefix · along

**The file's standing.** ONE v361, received in Equilibria Definitions v363: every numbered naming opens at its parity: the odd at co-, along self and chaining, *along changing between linear parallel sequencing momentaries*; the even at bi-, across society and tunnelling, *across changing between parallel linear sequencing momentaries*. ### GGG05 · The identity's home in science and engineering is the turn that removes a coupling between two: principal axes, Mohr's circle, Jacobi's rotations · across

**The files' standing.** Natural Engineering v345a; Natural Physics v348; Natural Mathematics v333. Kit · The Concentric Rings in Motion, EEE01 to EEE05.

**The coupling.** Wherever two quantities carry a coupling between them, a symmetric pair `[[a, b], [b, c]]`, turning the axes by `θ` with `tan 2θ = 2b / (a − c)` removes the coupling `b`; the identity then gives `tan θ` from `tan 2θ`. That is the principal-stress angle on Mohr's circle (1882), the principal axes of inertia, the orientation of a covariance ellipse in principal component analysis and of an image's moments, the rotation that removes the `xy` term from a conic, and each step of Jacobi's eigenvalue method (1846), still used for eigenvalues and singular values. When the two are equal, `a = c`, the turn is 45°, the identity's pole. `tangent_double_angle_v365.py`, part 6: a pair `5, 1` coupled by `2` turns 22.5° to leave no coupling, and an equal pair `3, 3` turns 45°. Its other homes: the rational circle, `t = tan(x/2)` giving `sin x = 2t / (1 + t²)` and `cos x = (1 − t²) / (1 + t²)`, which integrates rational functions of sine and cosine, draws exact circles in CAD as rational curves, and at `t = n/m` gives every Pythagorean triple, `(m² − n², 2mn, m² + n²)`; and half-wave plates, which turn polarization by twice their own angle.

### GGG06 · In cryptography the identity is the doubling law of the circle group read in slopes, and doubling reads binary digits as elliptic-curve and modular arithmetic do · across

**The files' standing.** Natural Numbers v346c; Exhibit ONE v345a; Natural Engineering v345a. Kit · The Concentric Rings in Motion, EEE04, doubling reads the binary digits.

**The session's standing.** *is this used in cryptography or logic.*

**The coupling.** The identity is not itself a cipher. Its structure runs through cryptography at four places. The points of the circle `x² + y² = 1` form a group whose doubling is `(x, y) → (x² − y², 2xy)`, the double angle, and in slopes `t = y/x` that doubling is `t → 2t / (1 − t²)`. Over finite fields the same circle groups and their Lucas and Chebyshev multiple-angle laws carry public-key systems, LUC (Smith and Lennon, 1993) and torus-based cryptography (Rubin and Silverberg, 2003), whose hardness is the discrete logarithm in a field. Elliptic-curve cryptography doubles a point by drawing the tangent line at it, and computes `kP` by doubling at every binary digit of `k` and adding at each 1, which is modular exponentiation's square-and-multiply on a curve: doubling reads the digits one at a time, as the tangent's sign did at EEE04. And the chaotic side of doubling, `x → 4x(1 − x)`, is `sin² 2θ = 4 sin²θ cos²θ`, proposed by Ulam and von Neumann in 1947 to make random numbers; chaos-based ciphers built on such maps, and Chebyshev-polynomial public keys over the reals, have been broken (Bergamo and others, 2005).

**The surplus.** **So in cryptography the identity lives as doubling, and doubling is where the binary digits are read**: the circle's doubling law, the tangent line's doubling on an elliptic curve, and square-and-multiply are one move at three groups. Its security never comes from the identity; it comes from the discrete logarithm in the group the doubling runs in. In logic the identity's face is the doubling map's reading of one binary digit per doubling, the shift.

Held as one store of the whole, the Peres–Mermin square is six exclusive-or constraints on nine signs: each row and each column read as its own context answers, four ways of eight, and a store of all nine meets none, 0 of 512, since read along the nine carry parity 0 and read across 1 (MMM03).

Every odd n goes to an even `3n + 1`, so odd always passes to even, the odd-even momentary forced, while even goes to either. With the halving folded into the odd step, the first run of odd steps from n is the count of n's trailing binary ones, and the first run of even steps its trailing noughts, at every n to `2^20`: odd forever is every digit one, which is −1, a fixed point, and even forever every digit nought, which is 0. Each cycle's sign is the sign of `2^m − 3^k`, m steps with k odd, so a positive cycle keeps its odd share below `log 2 / log 3`, 0.6309.
